C:\Users\fatha\Develop\UNILA-AQI\unila_aqi\lib\presentation\widgets\chart\monthly_trend_chart.dart

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/core/constants/colors.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/core/constants/parameter_colors.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/core/services/api_service.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/core/utils/helpers.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/data/models/monthly_trend.dart';

class MonthlyTrendChart extends StatefulWidget {
  final String roomId;

  const MonthlyTrendChart({super.key, required this.roomId});

  @override
  State<MonthlyTrendChart> createState() => _MonthlyTrendChartState();
}

class _MonthlyTrendChartState extends State<MonthlyTrendChart> {
  late int _year;
  late int _month;
  String _parameter = 'aqi';

  bool _isLoading = false;
  String _error = '';
  MonthlyTrendResponse? _response;

  static const Map<String, String> _paramLabels = {
    'aqi': 'AQI',
    'pm25': 'PM2.5',
    'pm10': 'PM10',
    'co': 'CO',
    'so2': 'SO2',
    'o3': 'O3',
    'no2': 'NO2',
    'temperature': 'Suhu',
    'humidity': 'Kelembaban',
  };

  static const Map<String, String> _paramUnits = {
    'aqi': '',
    'pm25': 'μg/m³',
    'pm10': 'μg/m³',
    'co': 'μg/m³',
    'so2': 'μg/m³',
    'o3': 'μg/m³',
    'no2': 'μg/m³',
    'temperature': '°C',
    'humidity': '%',
  };

  static const List<String> _paramOrder = [
    'aqi', 'pm25', 'pm10', 'co', 'so2', 'o3', 'no2',
    'temperature', 'humidity',
  ];

  static const List<String> _monthNames = [
    'Januari', 'Februari', 'Maret', 'April', 'Mei', 'Juni',
    'Juli', 'Agustus', 'September', 'Oktober', 'November', 'Desember',
  ];

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    final now = DateTime.now();
    _year = now.year;
    _month = now.month;
    _loadData();
  }

  Future<void> _loadData() async {
    if (!mounted) return;
    final hadData = _response != null && _response!.hasAnyData;

    setState(() {
      _isLoading = !hadData;
      _error = '';
    });

    final result = await ApiService()
        .getMonthlyTrend(widget.roomId, year: _year, month: _month);

    if (!mounted) return;

    if (result is Map && result['success'] == true) {
      setState(() {
        _response = MonthlyTrendResponse.fromJson(
          Map<String, dynamic>.from(result),
        );
        _isLoading = false;
      });
    } else {
      final errStr = (result is Map) ? result['error']?.toString() ?? '' : '';
      setState(() {
        _isLoading = false;
        _error = Helpers.isNetworkError(errStr)
            ? 'Tidak dapat memuat trend bulanan karena server tidak dapat dihubungi.'
            : 'Gagal memuat trend bulanan.';
      });
      if (Helpers.isNetworkError(errStr)) {
        Helpers.showServerError(context);
      } else {
        Helpers.showSnackBar(context, 'Gagal memuat trend bulanan',
            isError: true);
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _changeMonth(int delta) async {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    int newMonth = _month + delta;
    int newYear = _year;

    if (newMonth < 1) {
      newMonth = 12;
      newYear -= 1;
    } else if (newMonth > 12) {
      newMonth = 1;
      newYear += 1;
    }

    final candidate = DateTime(newYear, newMonth);
    final current = DateTime(now.year, now.month);
    if (candidate.isAfter(current)) return;

    setState(() {
      _year = newYear;
      _month = newMonth;
    });
    await _loadData();
  }

  // ✅ BARU: Menggunakan DatePicker untuk memilih bulan/tahun
  Future<void> _pickMonthYear(BuildContext context) async {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    
    // Kita gunakan showDatePicker tapi kita sembunyikan harinya (hanya ambil bulan & tahun)
    final DateTime? picked = await showDatePicker(
      context: context,
      initialDate: DateTime(_year, _month),
      firstDate: DateTime(now.year - 5, 1), // Batas 5 tahun ke belakang
      lastDate: now, // Batas maksimal hari ini
      helpText: 'Pilih Bulan & Tahun',
      initialDatePickerMode: DatePickerMode.year, // Langsung buka mode pilih tahun
    );

    if (picked != null) {
      // Cek jika user memilih bulan/tahun yang sama, tidak perlu load ulang
      if (picked.year == _year && picked.month == _month) return;
      
      setState(() {
        _year = picked.year;
        _month = picked.month;
      });
      await _loadData();
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      width: double.infinity,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.05),
            blurRadius: 8,
            offset: const Offset(0, 3),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              const Icon(Icons.bar_chart_rounded,
                  color: Colors.blue, size: 18),
              const SizedBox(width: 6),
              const Expanded(
                child: Text(
                  'TREND HARIAN BULANAN',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 14,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              IconButton(
                onPressed: _isLoading ? null : _loadData,
                icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh, size: 20),
                tooltip: 'Refresh trend bulanan',
                splashRadius: 20,
                padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                constraints: const BoxConstraints(),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          _buildMonthYearSelector(),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          _buildParameterChips(),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          if (_isLoading)
            const SizedBox(
              height: 350,
              child: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
            )
          else if (_error.isNotEmpty)
            _buildErrorState()
          else if (_response == null || !_response!.hasAnyData)
            _buildEmptyState()
          else
            ..._buildAllCharts(),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  // ✅ DIUBAH: Tampilan selector dibuat persis seperti di HistoryChart
  Widget _buildMonthYearSelector() {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: AppColors.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
        border: Border.all(color: AppColors.border),
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.chevron_left),
            onPressed: () => _changeMonth(-1),
            tooltip: 'Bulan sebelumnya',
          ),
          Expanded(
            child: InkWell(
              onTap: () => _pickMonthYear(context), // Memanggil DatePicker
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
              child: Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8),
                child: Column(
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      '${_monthNames[_month - 1]} $_year',
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 15,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                        fontFamily: 'Inter',
                      ),
                      textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.chevron_right),
            onPressed: () => _changeMonth(1),
            tooltip: 'Bulan berikutnya',
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildParameterChips() {
    return SizedBox(
      height: 40,
      child: ListView.builder(
        scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
        itemCount: _paramOrder.length,
        itemBuilder: (context, index) {
          final p = _paramOrder[index];
          final isSelected = _parameter == p;
          final color = ParameterColors.of(p);

          return GestureDetector(
            onTap: () => setState(() => _parameter = p),
            child: Container(
              margin: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 6),
              padding:
                  const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 6),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: isSelected
                    ? color
                    : AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.08),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
              ),
              child: Row(
                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                children: [
                  Container(
                    width: 8,
                    height: 8,
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: isSelected ? Colors.white : color,
                      shape: BoxShape.circle,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 6),
                  Center(
                    child: Text(
                      _paramLabels[p] ?? p,
                      style: TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 12,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                        color: isSelected ? Colors.white : color,
                        fontFamily: 'Inter',
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  List<Widget> _buildAllCharts() {
    final resp = _response!;
    final unit = _paramUnits[_parameter] ?? '';
    final label = _paramLabels[_parameter] ?? _parameter;

    final baseColor = ParameterColors.of(_parameter);

    return [
      _MonthlyBarChart(
        title: 'Tertinggi',
        subtitle: 'Nilai $label tertinggi dalam bulan ini',
        days: resp.days,
        parameter: _parameter,
        metric: 'max',
        unit: unit,
        color: baseColor,
      ),
      const SizedBox(height: 12),
      _MonthlyBarChart(
        title: 'Rata-rata',
        subtitle: 'Rata-rata nilai $label harian dalam bulan ini',
        days: resp.days,
        parameter: _parameter,
        metric: 'avg',
        unit: unit,
        color: baseColor,
      ),
      const SizedBox(height: 12),
      _MonthlyBarChart(
        title: 'Terendah',
        subtitle: 'Nilai $label terendah dari semua hari sejenis dalam bulan ini',
        days: resp.days,
        parameter: _parameter,
        metric: 'min',
        unit: unit,
        color: baseColor,
      ),
    ];
  }

  Widget _buildErrorState() {
    return SizedBox(
      height: 350, 
      child: Center(
        child: Column(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
          children: [
            const Icon(Icons.error_outline, size: 48, color: AppColors.textHint),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            Text(
              _error,
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              style: const TextStyle(
                fontSize: 12.5,
                color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                fontFamily: 'Inter',
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            ElevatedButton.icon(
              onPressed: _loadData,
              icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh, size: 16),
              label: const Text('Coba Lagi'),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildEmptyState() {
    return SizedBox(
      height: 350, 
      child: Center(
        child: Column(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
          children: [
            const Icon(Icons.bar_chart_outlined,
                size: 48, color: AppColors.textHint),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            Text(
              'Tidak ada data di ${_monthNames[_month - 1]} $_year',
              style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textHint),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// BAR CHART PER METRIK
// ============================================================
class _MonthlyBarChart extends StatelessWidget {
  final String title;
  final String subtitle;
  final List<MonthlyDayData> days;
  final String parameter;
  final String metric;
  final String unit;
  final Color color;

  const _MonthlyBarChart({
    required this.title,
    required this.subtitle,
    required this.days,
    required this.parameter,
    required this.metric,
    required this.unit,
    required this.color,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final values = days
        .map((d) => d.values[parameter]?.byMetric(metric))
        .toList(growable: false);

    final maxVal = values
        .whereType<double>()
        .fold<double>(0.0, (a, b) => b > a ? b : a);
    final chartMax = maxVal > 0 ? maxVal * 1.15 : 1.0;

    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(10, 10, 10, 8),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: color.withOpacity(0.04),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
        border: Border.all(color: color.withOpacity(0.25)),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              Container(
                width: 8,
                height: 8,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(color: color, shape: BoxShape.circle),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 6),
              Text(
                title,
                style: TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 13,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                  color: color,
                  fontFamily: 'Inter',
                ),
              ),
              if (unit.isNotEmpty) ...[
                const SizedBox(width: 4),
                Text(
                  '($unit)',
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 10,
                    color: AppColors.textHint,
                    fontFamily: 'Inter',
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 2),
          Text(
            subtitle,
            style: const TextStyle(
              fontSize: 10.5,
              color: AppColors.textSecondary,
              fontFamily: 'Inter',
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          SizedBox(
            height: 140,
            child: LayoutBuilder(
              builder: (context, constraints) {
                final labelSpace = 30.0;
                final barAreaHeight = constraints.maxHeight - labelSpace;

                return Row(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
                  children: List.generate(days.length, (i) {
                    final day = days[i];
                    final value = values[i];
                    final hasData = value != null;

                    final barHeight = hasData
                        ? (value / chartMax) * barAreaHeight
                        : 0.0;

                    return Expanded(
                      child: Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 2),
                        child: Column(
                          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.end,
                          children: [
                            SizedBox(
                              height: 14,
                              child: FittedBox(
                                fit: BoxFit.scaleDown,
                                child: Text(
                                  hasData
                                      ? _formatValue(value, parameter)
                                      : '—',
                                  style: TextStyle(
                                    fontSize: 9.5,
                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                                    color: hasData
                                        ? AppColors.textPrimary
                                        : AppColors.textHint,
                                    fontFamily: 'Inter',
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(height: 2),
                            Container(
                              height: hasData && barHeight > 4
                                  ? barHeight
                                  : 4,
                              width: double.infinity,
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: hasData
                                    ? color
                                    : Colors.grey.withOpacity(0.3),
                                borderRadius: const BorderRadius.vertical(
                                  top: Radius.circular(4),
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(height: 4),
                            SizedBox(
                              height: 12,
                              child: FittedBox(
                                fit: BoxFit.scaleDown,
                                child: Text(
                                  _dayShort(day.dayName),
                                  style: const TextStyle(
                                    fontSize: 9.5,
                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                                    color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                                    fontFamily: 'Inter',
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                    );
                  }),
                );
              },
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  static String _formatValue(double v, String parameter) {
    if (parameter == 'aqi') return v.round().toString();
    if (parameter == 'humidity') return v.round().toString();
    if (parameter == 'co') return v.round().toString();
    return v.toStringAsFixed(1);
  }

  static String _dayShort(String dayName) {
    const map = {
      'Senin': 'Sen',
      'Selasa': 'Sel',
      'Rabu': 'Rab',
      'Kamis': 'Kam',
      'Jumat': 'Jum',
      'Sabtu': 'Sab',
      'Minggu': 'Min',
    };
    return map[dayName] ?? dayName;
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import 'room_card.dart';

class BuildingSection extends StatelessWidget {
  final String buildingName;
  final List<Room> rooms;
  final Function(Room)? onRoomTap;
  
  const BuildingSection({
    super.key,
    required this.buildingName,
    required this.rooms,
    this.onRoomTap,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 24),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          
          // Building Header
// Building Header
Container(
  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 8),
  decoration: BoxDecoration(
    color: Theme.of(context).primaryColor.withOpacity(0.1),
    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
  ),
  child: IntrinsicHeight(
    child: Row(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch, 
      children: [
        // ✅ Ikon sekarang di tengah secara vertikal
        Align(
          alignment: Alignment.center, // Ubah dari topLeft ke center
          child: const Icon(Icons.location_city, size: 20), // Padding dihapus
        ),
        const SizedBox(width: 8),
        
        // Nama gedung (bisa wrap ke bawah)
        Expanded(
          child: Text(
            buildingName,
            style: const TextStyle(
              fontSize: 18,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
            ),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(width: 8),
        
        // Chip jumlah ruangan (tetap di tengah)
        Align(
          alignment: Alignment.center, 
          child: Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 2),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Theme.of(context).primaryColor,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
            ),
            child: Text(
              '${rooms.length} ruangan',
              style: const TextStyle(
                fontSize: 12,
                color: Colors.white,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    ),
  ),
),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          // Rooms List
          ListView.separated(
            physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
            shrinkWrap: true,
            itemCount: rooms.length,
            separatorBuilder: (context, index) => const SizedBox(height: 12),
            itemBuilder: (context, index) {
              final room = rooms[index];
              return RoomCard(
                room: room,
                onTap: () {
                  if (onRoomTap != null) {
                    onRoomTap!(room);
                  }
                },
              );
            },
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

  Widget _buildMenuItem({
    required IconData icon,
    required String label,
    VoidCallback? onTap,
  }) {
    return ListTile(
      leading: Icon(icon),
      title: Text(
        label,
        style: const TextStyle(
          fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
        ),
      ),
      onTap: onTap,
      enableFeedback: false,
    );
  }

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../../data/models/room.dart';
import '../../../core/utils/helpers.dart';

class RoomCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final Room room;
  final VoidCallback? onTap;

  const RoomCard({
    super.key,
    required this.room,
    this.onTap,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final aqi = room.currentAQI;
    final aqiColor = Helpers.getAQIColor(aqi);
    final aqiLabel = Helpers.getAQILabel(aqi);
    final hasAnyData = room.hasAnyData;

    return Card(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8, horizontal: 0),
      elevation: 2,
      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
      child: InkWell(
        onTap: onTap,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              // Header
              Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                children: [
                  Expanded(
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Text(
                          room.name,
                          style: const TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 18,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                            color: Color(0xFF212529),
                          ),
                          overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 2),
                        Text(
                          room.buildingName,
                          style: const TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 12,
                            color: Color(0xFF6C757D),
                          ),
                          overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  Container(
                    padding:
                        const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: aqiColor.withOpacity(0.1),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                      border: Border.all(color: aqiColor),
                    ),
                    child: Text(
                      aqiLabel,
                      style: TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 12,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                        color: aqiColor,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),

              const SizedBox(height: 16),

              // AQI Display — no_data & error jadi grey
              Container(
                width: double.infinity,
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: aqiColor,
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                ),
                child: Column(
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      aqi?.toString() ?? '—',
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 64,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                        color: Colors.white,
                        height: 0.9,
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 4),
                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                          horizontal: 20, vertical: 6),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.2),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                      ),
                      child: Text(
                        aqi == null ? 'TIDAK ADA DATA' : 'INDEKS KUALITAS UDARA',
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          color: Colors.white,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                          fontSize: 12,
                          letterSpacing: 0.5,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),

              const SizedBox(height: 20),

              // Grid param dinamis — card yang null otomatis hilang
              GridView.count(
                physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
                shrinkWrap: true,
                crossAxisCount: 3,
                mainAxisSpacing: 12,
                crossAxisSpacing: 12,
                childAspectRatio: 1.1,
                children: [
                  if (room.currentData.hasPM25)
                    _buildParameterCell(
                      label: 'PM2.5',
                      value: room.currentData.pm25!.toStringAsFixed(1),
                      unit: 'µg/m³',
                      status: Helpers.getPM25Status(room.currentData.pm25),
                      color: Helpers.getPM25Color(room.currentData.pm25),
                    ),
                  if (room.currentData.hasPM10)
                    _buildParameterCell(
                      label: 'PM10',
                      value: room.currentData.pm10!.toStringAsFixed(1),
                      unit: 'µg/m³',
                      status: Helpers.getPM10Status(room.currentData.pm10),
                      color: Helpers.getPM10Color(room.currentData.pm10),
                    ),
                  if (room.currentData.hasCO)
                    _buildParameterCell(
                      label: 'CO',
                      value: room.currentData.co!.round().toString(),
                      unit: 'µg/m³',
                      status: Helpers.getCOStatus(room.currentData.co),
                      color: Helpers.getCOColor(room.currentData.co),
                    ),
                  if (room.currentData.hasSO2)
                    _buildParameterCell(
                      label: 'SO2',
                      value: room.currentData.so2!.toStringAsFixed(1),
                      unit: 'µg/m³',
                      status: Helpers.getSO2Status(room.currentData.so2),
                      color: Helpers.getSO2Color(room.currentData.so2),
                    ),
                  if (room.currentData.hasO3)
                    _buildParameterCell(
                      label: 'O3',
                      value: room.currentData.o3!.toStringAsFixed(1),
                      unit: 'µg/m³',
                      status: Helpers.getO3Status(room.currentData.o3),
                      color: Helpers.getO3Color(room.currentData.o3),
                    ),
                  if (room.currentData.hasNO2)
                    _buildParameterCell(
                      label: 'NO2',
                      value: room.currentData.no2!.toStringAsFixed(1),
                      unit: 'µg/m³',
                      status: Helpers.getNO2Status(room.currentData.no2),
                      color: Helpers.getNO2Color(room.currentData.no2),
                    ),
                  if (room.currentData.hasTemperature)
                    _buildParameterCell(
                      label: 'SUHU',
                      value: room.currentData.temperature!.toStringAsFixed(1),
                      unit: '°C',
                      status: Helpers.getTemperatureStatus(
                          room.currentData.temperature),
                      color: Helpers.getTemperatureColor(
                          room.currentData.temperature),
                    ),
                  if (room.currentData.hasHumidity)
                    _buildParameterCell(
                      label: 'LEMBAB',
                      value: room.currentData.humidity!.round().toString(),
                      unit: '%',
                      status: Helpers.getHumidityStatus(
                          room.currentData.humidity),
                      color: Helpers.getHumidityColor(
                          room.currentData.humidity),
                    ),
                  _buildLastUpdateCell(room),
                ],
              ),

              const SizedBox(height: 12),

              // Footer — semua ruangan kini bersumber dari device IoT
              Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  
                  if (!hasAnyData && aqi == null)
                    Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 8),
                      child: Text(
                        'Belum ada data dari perangkat',
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 10,
                          color: Colors.grey[600],
                          fontStyle: FontStyle.italic,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildParameterCell({
    required String label,
    required String value,
    required String unit,
    required String status,
    required Color color,
  }) {
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: color,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        boxShadow: const [
          BoxShadow(color: Colors.black12, blurRadius: 4, offset: Offset(0, 2)),
        ],
      ),
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
          children: [
            Text(
              label,
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 10,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.9),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 4),
            Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.baseline,
              textBaseline: TextBaseline.alphabetic,
              children: [
                Text(
                  value,
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 16,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                    color: Colors.white,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 2),
                Text(
                  unit,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 9,
                    color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.8),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 6),
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6, vertical: 2),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.2),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
              ),
              child: Text(
                status.length > 10 ? '${status.substring(0, 10)}...' : status,
                style: const TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 9,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                  color: Colors.white,
                ),
                overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                maxLines: 1,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildLastUpdateCell(Room room) {
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: const Color(0xFFF8F9FA),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        border: Border.all(color: const Color(0xFFE9ECEF)),
      ),
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
        child: Builder(
          builder: (context) {
            final updateInfo =
                Helpers.formatCompactLastUpdate(room.currentData.updatedAt);
            final label = updateInfo['label'] ?? '';
            final time = updateInfo['time'] ?? '';
            final topLabel = label == 'HARI INI' ? 'UPDATE' : label;

            return Column(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
              children: [
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                Text(
                  topLabel,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 10,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                    color: Colors.grey[700],
                    letterSpacing: 0.3,
                  ),
                  textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                  maxLines: 1,
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                Text(
                  time,
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 16,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                    color: Color(0xFF212529),
                  ),
                  textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                  maxLines: 1,
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                ),
              ],
            );
          },
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

C:\Users\fatha\Develop\UNILA-AQI\unila_aqi\lib\presentation\widgets\common\server_status_indicator.dart

import 'dart:async';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../../core/services/server_status_service.dart';

class ServerStatusIndicator extends StatefulWidget {
  final Duration pollInterval;
  final double size;

  const ServerStatusIndicator({
    super.key,
    this.pollInterval = const Duration(seconds: 10),
    this.size = 10,
  });

  static Future<bool> checkNow() => ServerStatusService().checkNow();

  @override
  State<ServerStatusIndicator> createState() => _ServerStatusIndicatorState();
}

class _ServerStatusIndicatorState extends State<ServerStatusIndicator> {
  final ServerStatusService _service = ServerStatusService();
  Timer? _timer;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    // Check pertama kali instan
    _service.checkNow();
    // Polling berkala sebagai fallback (refresh button tetap jalan
    // lewat static helper).
    _timer = Timer.periodic(widget.pollInterval, (_) => _service.checkNow());
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _timer?.cancel();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ValueListenableBuilder<bool?>(
      valueListenable: _service.status,
      builder: (context, value, _) {
        final Color color;
        if (value == null) {
          color = Colors.grey;
        } else if (value == true) {
          color = Colors.green;
        } else {
          color = Colors.red;
        }

        return Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6),
          child: Center(
            child: AnimatedContainer(
              duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
              width: widget.size,
              height: widget.size,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: color,
                shape: BoxShape.circle,
                boxShadow: [
                  BoxShadow(
                    color: color.withOpacity(0.55),
                    blurRadius: 6,
                    spreadRadius: 1,
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_localizations/flutter_localizations.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/presentation/pages/admin/building_management.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/presentation/pages/admin/iot_management.dart';
import 'package:unila_aqi/presentation/pages/admin/room_management.dart';
import 'core/themes/app_theme.dart';
import 'presentation/providers/auth_provider.dart';
import 'presentation/providers/room_provider.dart';
import 'presentation/providers/building_provider.dart';
import 'presentation/pages/auth/mode_selection_screen.dart';
import 'presentation/pages/dashboard/dashboard_screen.dart';
import 'core/services/storage_service.dart';
import 'presentation/pages/admin/profile_management.dart';

void main() async {
  WidgetsFlutterBinding.ensureInitialized();

  // Initialize services
  await StorageService().init();

  runApp(
    MultiProvider(
      providers: [
        ChangeNotifierProvider(create: (_) => AuthProvider()),
        ChangeNotifierProvider(create: (_) => RoomProvider()),
        ChangeNotifierProvider(create: (_) => BuildingProvider()),
      ],
      child: const MyApp(),
    ),
  );
}

class MyApp extends StatelessWidget {
  const MyApp({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    // Initialize socket connection when app starts
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      final roomProvider = Provider.of<RoomProvider>(context, listen: false);
      // FIXED: catchError agar rejection dari initSocket tidak unhandled.
      roomProvider.initSocket().catchError((e) {
        debugPrint('❌ initSocket error: $e');
      });
    });

    return MaterialApp(
      title: 'UNILA Air Quality Index',
      debugShowCheckedModeBanner: false,
      theme: AppTheme.lightTheme,
      initialRoute: '/',

      // ✅ Localization: dukung Bahasa Indonesia
      localizationsDelegates: const [
        GlobalMaterialLocalizations.delegate,
        GlobalWidgetsLocalizations.delegate,
        GlobalCupertinoLocalizations.delegate,
      ],
      supportedLocales: const [
        Locale('id', 'ID'), // Bahasa Indonesia
        Locale('en', 'US'), // Fallback (opsional)
      ],
      locale: const Locale('id', 'ID'), // Default seluruh app ke Indonesia

      routes: {
        '/': (context) => const ModeSelectionScreen(),
        '/dashboard': (context) => const DashboardScreen(isAdminMode: false),
        '/admin-dashboard': (context) => const DashboardScreen(isAdminMode: true),
        '/admin/buildings': (context) => const BuildingManagementScreen(),
        '/admin/rooms': (context) => const RoomManagementScreen(),
        '/admin/iot-devices': (context) => const IoTManagementScreen(),
        '/admin/profile': (context) => const ProfileManagementScreen(),
      },
      // Enable smooth scrolling
      scrollBehavior: const MaterialScrollBehavior().copyWith(
        physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
      ),
      builder: (context, child) {
        return MediaQuery(
          data: MediaQuery.of(context).copyWith(
            textScaler: const TextScaler.linear(1.0),
          ),
          child: child ?? const SizedBox(),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

C:\Users\fatha\Develop\UNILA-AQI\unila-aqi-backend\src\controllers\authController.js

const User = require('../models/User');
const { generateToken, hashPassword, comparePassword } = require('../utils/auth');
const {
  generateResetCode,
  sendResetCodeEmail,
  sendEmailChangeCodeEmail
} = require('../utils/emailService');

// Register admin (untuk pertama kali)
const registerAdmin = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { username, password, email } = req.body;

    // Check if admin already exists
    const existingAdmin = await User.findOne({ role: 'admin' });
    if (existingAdmin) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Admin already registered'
      });
    }

    // Create new admin
    const hashedPassword = await hashPassword(password);
    
    const admin = new User({
      username,
      password: hashedPassword,
      email,
      role: 'admin'
    });

    await admin.save();

    // Generate token
    const token = generateToken(admin._id, admin.username, admin.role);

    res.status(201).json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Admin registered successfully',
      token,
      user: {
        id: admin._id,
        username: admin.username,
        role: admin.role,
        email: admin.email
      }
    });

  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Register error:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Server error',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
};

// Login
const login = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { username, password, role } = req.body;

    // Find user
    const user = await User.findOne({ username });
    if (!user) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Invalid credentials'
      });
    }

    // Check if user is active
    if (!user.isActive) {
      return res.status(403).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Account is disabled'
      });
    }

    // Check role (admin harus login sebagai admin, user sebagai user)
    if (role && user.role !== role) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Invalid role selection'
      });
    }

    // Verify password
    const isPasswordValid = await comparePassword(password, user.password);
    if (!isPasswordValid) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Invalid credentials'
      });
    }

    // Update last login
    user.lastLogin = new Date();
    await user.save();

    // Generate token
    const token = generateToken(user._id, user.username, user.role);

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Login successful',
      token,
      user: {
        id: user._id,
        username: user.username,
        role: user.role,
        email: user.email
      }
    });

  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Login error:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Server error',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
};

// Get current user profile
const getProfile = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const user = await User.findById(req.user.id).select('-password');
    
    if (!user) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'User not found'
      });
    }

    res.json({
      success: true,
      user
    });

  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Get profile error:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Server error'
    });
  }
};

// Check if admin is registered (untuk frontend)
const checkAdminRegistered = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const admin = await User.findOne({ role: 'admin' });
    
    res.json({
      success: true,
      isRegistered: !!admin
    });

  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Check admin error:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Server error'
    });
  }
};

const requestEmailChangeCode = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { newEmail } = req.body;

    if (!newEmail || !newEmail.trim()) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Email baru wajib diisi'
      });
    }

    const email = newEmail.trim().toLowerCase();
    const emailRegex = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;

    if (!emailRegex.test(email)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Format email tidak valid'
      });
    }

    const user = await User.findById(req.user.id);

    if (!user) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'User not found'
      });
    }

    const currentEmail = (user.email || '').trim().toLowerCase();

    if (currentEmail === email) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Email baru sama dengan email saat ini'
      });
    }

    const existingUser = await User.findOne({
      email,
      _id: { $ne: user._id }
    });

    if (existingUser) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Email sudah digunakan akun lain'
      });
    }

    const code = generateResetCode();
    const expiry = new Date(Date.now() + 15 * 60 * 1000);

    user.pendingEmail = email;
    user.emailChangeCode = code;
    user.emailChangeCodeExpiry = expiry;
    await user.save();

    try {
      await sendEmailChangeCodeEmail(email, code, user.username);
      console.log(`✅ Email change code sent to ${email}`);
    } catch (emailError) {
      console.error('❌ Email send error:', emailError);

      // FIXED: rollback — hapus semua field verifikasi agar user bisa
      // request ulang tanpa status "pendingEmail" yang menggantung.
      try {
        user.pendingEmail = undefined;
        user.emailChangeCode = undefined;
        user.emailChangeCodeExpiry = undefined;
        await user.save();
        console.log('↩️  Email change state rolled back');
      } catch (rollbackError) {
        console.error('❌ Rollback email change failed:', rollbackError.message);
      }

      return res.status(500).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Gagal mengirim email. Periksa koneksi server atau coba lagi.'
      });
    }

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Kode verifikasi telah dikirim ke email baru',
      email
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Request email change error:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Server error',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
};

// Update profile (username & email)
const updateProfile = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { username, email, emailChangeCode } = req.body;

    if (!username || !username.trim()) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Username is required'
      });
    }

    const user = await User.findById(req.user.id);

    if (!user) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'User not found'
      });
    }

    const currentEmail = (user.email || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    const requestedEmail = email !== undefined
      ? (email ? email.trim().toLowerCase() : '')
      : currentEmail;

    const emailChanged = requestedEmail !== currentEmail;

    // Validasi format email jika diisi
    if (requestedEmail) {
      const emailRegex = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;
      if (!emailRegex.test(requestedEmail)) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Invalid email format'
        });
      }
    }

    // Jika email berubah, wajib verifikasi kode
    if (emailChanged) {
      if (requestedEmail === '') {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Email baru wajib diisi'
        });
      }

      if (!emailChangeCode || !emailChangeCode.trim()) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Kode verifikasi email wajib diisi'
        });
      }

      if (!user.pendingEmail || user.pendingEmail !== requestedEmail) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Silakan minta kode verifikasi untuk email baru terlebih dahulu'
        });
      }

      if (!user.emailChangeCode || !user.emailChangeCodeExpiry) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Kode verifikasi tidak ditemukan. Silakan minta kode baru.'
        });
      }

      if (new Date() > new Date(user.emailChangeCodeExpiry)) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Kode verifikasi sudah kedaluwarsa. Silakan minta kode baru.'
        });
      }

      if (user.emailChangeCode !== emailChangeCode.trim()) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Kode verifikasi salah'
        });
      }
    }

    // Cek username unik
    if (username.trim() !== user.username) {
      const existing = await User.findOne({
        username: username.trim(),
        _id: { $ne: user._id }
      });

      if (existing) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Username already taken'
        });
      }
    }

    user.username = username.trim();

    if (emailChanged) {
      user.email = requestedEmail;

      // Bersihkan data verifikasi
      user.pendingEmail = undefined;
      user.emailChangeCode = undefined;
      user.emailChangeCodeExpiry = undefined;
    } else if (email !== undefined) {
      user.email = requestedEmail;
    }

    await user.save();

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Profile updated successfully',
      user: {
        id: user._id,
        username: user.username,
        email: user.email,
        role: user.role
      }
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Update profile error:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Server error',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
};

// Change password
const changePassword = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { oldPassword, newPassword } = req.body;

    if (!oldPassword || !newPassword) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Old and new password are required'
      });
    }

    if (newPassword.length < 8) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Password must be at least 8 characters'
      });
    }

    if (!/^(?=.*[a-zA-Z])(?=.*\d)/.test(newPassword)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Password must contain both letters and numbers'
      });
    }

    const user = await User.findById(req.user.id);
    if (!user) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'User not found'
      });
    }

    const isValid = await comparePassword(oldPassword, user.password);
    if (!isValid) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Old password is incorrect'
      });
    }

    if (oldPassword === newPassword) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'New password must be different from old password'
      });
    }

    user.password = await hashPassword(newPassword);
    await user.save();

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Password changed successfully'
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Change password error:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Server error',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
};

// ============ FITUR LUPA PASSWORD ============

// 1. Request reset password (kirim kode ke email)
const requestPasswordReset = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { email } = req.body;

    if (!email || !email.trim()) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Email wajib diisi',
      });
    }

    // Cari admin dengan email tersebut
    const user = await User.findOne({
      email: email.trim().toLowerCase(),
      role: 'admin',
    });

    if (!user) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Email tidak terdaftar sebagai admin',
      });
    }

    // Generate kode 6-digit
    const code = generateResetCode();
    const expiry = new Date(Date.now() + 15 * 60 * 1000); // 15 menit

    user.resetPasswordCode = code;
    user.resetPasswordCodeExpiry = expiry;
    await user.save();

    // Kirim email
    try {
      await sendResetCodeEmail(user.email, code, user.username);
      console.log(`✅ Reset code sent to ${user.email}`);
    } catch (emailError) {
      console.error('❌ Email send error:', emailError);

      // FIXED: rollback kode reset agar tidak menggantung saat email gagal.
      try {
        user.resetPasswordCode = undefined;
        user.resetPasswordCodeExpiry = undefined;
        await user.save();
        console.log('↩️  Reset password state rolled back');
      } catch (rollbackError) {
        console.error('❌ Rollback reset password failed:', rollbackError.message);
      }

      return res.status(500).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Gagal mengirim email. Periksa koneksi server atau coba lagi.',
      });
    }

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Kode verifikasi telah dikirim ke email Anda',
      email: user.email,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Request password reset error:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Server error',
      error: error.message,
    });
  }
};

// 2. Reset password dengan kode verifikasi
const resetPasswordWithCode = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { email, code, newPassword } = req.body;

    if (!email || !code || !newPassword) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Email, kode, dan password baru wajib diisi',
      });
    }

    if (newPassword.length < 8) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Password minimal 8 karakter',
      });
    }

    if (!/^(?=.*[a-zA-Z])(?=.*\d)/.test(newPassword)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Password harus mengandung huruf dan angka',
      });
    }

    const user = await User.findOne({
      email: email.trim().toLowerCase(),
      role: 'admin',
    });

    if (!user) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Email tidak terdaftar sebagai admin',
      });
    }

    if (!user.resetPasswordCode || !user.resetPasswordCodeExpiry) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Kode verifikasi tidak ditemukan. Silakan minta kode baru.',
      });
    }

    if (new Date() > new Date(user.resetPasswordCodeExpiry)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Kode sudah kedaluwarsa. Silakan minta kode baru.',
      });
    }

    if (user.resetPasswordCode !== code.trim()) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Kode verifikasi salah',
      });
    }

    // Update password
    user.password = await hashPassword(newPassword);
    user.resetPasswordCode = undefined;
    user.resetPasswordCodeExpiry = undefined;
    await user.save();

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Password berhasil direset',
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Reset password error:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Server error',
      error: error.message,
    });
  }
};

// 3. Kirim ulang kode
const resendResetCode = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { email } = req.body;

    if (!email || !email.trim()) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Email wajib diisi',
      });
    }

    const user = await User.findOne({
      email: email.trim().toLowerCase(),
      role: 'admin',
    });

    if (!user) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Email tidak terdaftar sebagai admin',
      });
    }

    // Generate kode baru
    const code = generateResetCode();
    const expiry = new Date(Date.now() + 15 * 60 * 1000);

    user.resetPasswordCode = code;
    user.resetPasswordCodeExpiry = expiry;
    await user.save();

    try {
      await sendResetCodeEmail(user.email, code, user.username);
      console.log(`✅ Reset code resent to ${user.email}`);
    } catch (emailError) {
      console.error('❌ Email send error:', emailError);

      // FIXED: rollback kode reset agar tidak menggantung saat email gagal.
      try {
        user.resetPasswordCode = undefined;
        user.resetPasswordCodeExpiry = undefined;
        await user.save();
        console.log('↩️  Resend reset code state rolled back');
      } catch (rollbackError) {
        console.error('❌ Rollback resend failed:', rollbackError.message);
      }

      return res.status(500).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Gagal mengirim email. Coba lagi nanti.',
      });
    }

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Kode verifikasi baru telah dikirim ke email',
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Resend code error:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Server error',
      error: error.message,
    });
  }
};

const { verifyToken } = require('../utils/auth');

const authMiddleware = (req, res, next) => {
  // Get token from header
  const token = req.header('Authorization')?.replace('Bearer ', '');

  if (!token) {
    return res.status(401).json({ 
      success: false, 
      message: 'Access denied. No token provided.' 
    });
  }

  try {
    // Verify token
    const decoded = verifyToken(token);
    
    if (!decoded) {
      return res.status(401).json({ 
        success: false, 
        message: 'Invalid or expired token.' 
      });
    }

    // Add user info to request
    req.user = decoded;
    next();
  } catch (error) {
    return res.status(401).json({ 
      success: false, 
      message: 'Invalid token.' 
    });
  }
};

const adminMiddleware = (req, res, next) => {
  if (req.user.role !== 'admin') {
    return res.status(403).json({ 
      success: false, 
      message: 'Access denied. Admin only.' 
    });
  }
  next();
};

module.exports = { authMiddleware, adminMiddleware };

const mongoose = require('mongoose');

const sensorDataSchema = new mongoose.Schema({
  roomId: {
    type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
    ref: 'Room',
    required: true
  },
  roomName: { type: String, required: true },
  buildingName: { type: String, required: true },
  aqi: { type: Number, default: null },
  pm25: { type: Number, default: null },
  pm10: { type: Number, default: null },
  co: { type: Number, default: null },
  so2: { type: Number, default: null },
  o3: { type: Number, default: null },
  no2: { type: Number, default: null },
  temperature: { type: Number, default: null },
  humidity: { type: Number, default: null },
  category: {
    type: String,
    enum: ['baik', 'sedang', 'tidak_sehat', 'sangat_tidak_sehat', 'berbahaya', 'error', 'no_data'],
    required: true
  },
  
  isAnomaly: {
    type: Boolean,
    default: false,
    index: true,
  },
  anomalyType: {
    type: String,
    default: null,
  },

  timestamp: {
    type: Date,
    default: Date.now,
    index: true
  }
});

sensorDataSchema.index({ roomId: 1, timestamp: -1 });
sensorDataSchema.index({ timestamp: -1 });
sensorDataSchema.index({ roomId: 1, timestamp: 1, isAnomaly: 1 });

module.exports = mongoose.model('SensorData', sensorDataSchema);

const mongoose = require('mongoose');

const userSchema = new mongoose.Schema({
  username: {
    type: String,

    type: String,
    trim: true,
    lowercase: true
  },

  trim: true,

},

    default: Date.now
  },

  },

});

const express = require('express');
const router = express.Router();
const Building = require('../models/Building');
const Room = require('../models/Room');
const { authMiddleware, adminMiddleware } = require('../middleware/authMiddleware');

// GET all buildings (public)
router.get('/', async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const buildings = await Building.find().sort({ name: 1 });

    res.json({
      success: true,
      count: buildings.length,
      data: buildings
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error fetching buildings:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Gagal memuat data gedung'
    });
  }
});

// GET single building (public)
router.get('/:id', async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const building = await Building.findById(req.params.id);

    if (!building) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Gedung tidak ditemukan'
      });
    }

    res.json({
      success: true,
      data: building
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error fetching building:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Gagal memuat data gedung'
    });
  }
});

// POST create building (admin only)
router.post('/', authMiddleware, adminMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { name, code, description } = req.body;

    if (!name || !name.trim()) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Nama gedung wajib diisi'
      });
    }

    // Cek duplikat code HANYA jika code diisi
    if (code && code.trim()) {
      const existingCode = await Building.findOne({
        code: code.toUpperCase().trim()
      });
      if (existingCode) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Kode gedung sudah digunakan. Gunakan kode lain atau kosongkan.'
        });
      }
    }

    // ✅ FIX: jangan set field `code` sama sekali jika kosong
    const buildingData = {
      name: name.trim(),
      roomCount: 0,
      createdAt: new Date(),
      updatedAt: new Date()
    };

    if (code && code.trim()) {
      buildingData.code = code.toUpperCase().trim();
    }
    if (description && description.trim()) {
      buildingData.description = description.trim();
    }

    const building = new Building(buildingData);
    await building.save();

    res.status(201).json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Gedung berhasil dibuat',
      data: building
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error creating building:', error);

    if (error.code === 11000) {
      const dupField = Object.keys(error.keyPattern || {})[0];
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: dupField === 'code'
          ? 'Kode gedung sudah digunakan. Gunakan kode lain atau kosongkan.'
          : 'Data gedung sudah ada'
      });
    }

    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Gagal membuat gedung. Silakan coba lagi.'
    });
  }
});

// PUT update building (admin only)
router.put('/:id', authMiddleware, adminMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { name, code, description } = req.body;

    const building = await Building.findById(req.params.id);
    if (!building) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Gedung tidak ditemukan'
      });
    }

    const oldName = building.name;

    // Cek duplikat code jika berubah
    if (code && code.trim() && code.toUpperCase().trim() !== building.code) {
      const existing = await Building.findOne({
        code: code.toUpperCase().trim(),
        _id: { $ne: building._id }
      });
      if (existing) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Kode gedung sudah digunakan. Gunakan kode lain atau kosongkan.'
        });
      }
    }

    if (name && name.trim()) building.name = name.trim();

    if (code !== undefined) {
      if (code && code.trim()) {
        building.code = code.toUpperCase().trim();
      } else {
        // ✅ FIX: benar-benar unset field agar tidak tersimpan sebagai `null`
        building.set('code', undefined);
      }
    }

    if (description !== undefined) {
      building.description = description && description.trim()
        ? description.trim()
        : undefined;
    }
    building.updatedAt = new Date();

    await building.save();

    // Sinkronkan buildingName di semua Room terkait saat nama berubah
    if (oldName !== building.name) {
      await Room.updateMany(
        { building: building._id },
        { $set: { buildingName: building.name } }
      );
    }

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Gedung berhasil diperbarui',
      data: building
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error updating building:', error);

    if (error.code === 11000) {
      const dupField = Object.keys(error.keyPattern || {})[0];
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: dupField === 'code'
          ? 'Kode gedung sudah digunakan. Gunakan kode lain atau kosongkan.'
          : 'Data gedung sudah ada'
      });
    }

    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Gagal memperbarui gedung. Silakan coba lagi.'
    });
  }
});

// DELETE building (admin only)
router.delete('/:id', authMiddleware, adminMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const building = await Building.findById(req.params.id);
    if (!building) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Gedung tidak ditemukan'
      });
    }

    // Cegah hapus gedung yang masih memiliki ruangan
    const roomCount = await Room.countDocuments({ building: building._id });
    if (roomCount > 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: `Gedung tidak dapat dihapus karena masih memiliki ${roomCount} ruangan`
      });
    }

    await building.deleteOne();

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Gedung berhasil dihapus'
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error deleting building:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Gagal menghapus gedung. Silakan coba lagi.'
    });
  }
});

module.exports = router;

const express = require('express');
const router = express.Router();
const IoTDevice = require('../models/IoTDevice');
const { authMiddleware, adminMiddleware } = require('../middleware/authMiddleware');

// GET all IoT devices
router.get('/', authMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const live = req.query.live === 'true';

    let devices = await IoTDevice.find().sort({ name: 1 });

    // ✅ Bila ?live=true → lakukan quick-check status online/offline
    // sebelum mengembalikan data, sehingga status di frontend selalu fresh.
    if (live && devices.length > 0) {
      try {
        const iotService = require('../services/iotService');
        await iotService.checkDeviceStatus(devices);

        // Re-fetch untuk dapat status yang sudah di-update
        devices = await IoTDevice.find().sort({ name: 1 });
      } catch (checkErr) {
        console.warn('⚠️ Live status check failed:', checkErr.message);
        // Tetap lanjut dengan data cached bila check gagal
      }
    }

    res.json({
      success: true,
      count: devices.length,
      data: devices
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Terjadi kesalahan ketika mengambil data device iot',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
});

// GET single IoT device
router.get('/:id', authMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const device = await IoTDevice.findById(req.params.id);

    if (!device) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Device IoT tidak ditemukan'
      });
    }

    res.json({
      success: true,
      data: device
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Terjadi kesalahan ketika mengambil data device iot',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
});

// POST create IoT device (admin only)
router.post('/', authMiddleware, adminMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { name, description, apiEndpoint } = req.body;

    if (!name) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Nama device diperlukan'
      });
    }

    if (!apiEndpoint) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'API endpoint dibutuhkan untuk device IoT'
      });
    }

    try {
      new URL(apiEndpoint);
    } catch (error) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Format URL API endpoint tidak valid'
      });
    }

    const existingDevice = await IoTDevice.findOne({ name: name.trim() });
    if (existingDevice) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Nama Device ini sudah ada, silahkan ganti nama lain'
      });
    }

    const device = new IoTDevice({
      name: name.trim(),
      description: description?.trim(),
      apiEndpoint: apiEndpoint.trim(),
      status: 'offline',
      createdAt: new Date(),
      updatedAt: new Date()
    });

    await device.save();

    res.status(201).json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Device iot berhasil dibuat',
      data: device
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Terjadi kesalahan ketika membuat device iot',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
});

// PUT update IoT device (admin only)
router.put('/:id', authMiddleware, adminMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { name, description, apiEndpoint } = req.body;

    const device = await IoTDevice.findById(req.params.id);

    if (!device) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Device IoT tidak ditemukan'
      });
    }

    if (apiEndpoint) {
      try {
        new URL(apiEndpoint);
      } catch (error) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Format URL API endpoint tidak valid'
        });
      }
    }

    if (name && name !== device.name) {
      const existingDevice = await IoTDevice.findOne({
        name: name.trim(),
        _id: { $ne: device._id }
      });

      if (existingDevice) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Nama Device ini sudah ada, silahkan ganti nama lain'
        });
      }
    }

    if (name) device.name = name.trim();
    if (description !== undefined) device.description = description?.trim();
    if (apiEndpoint) device.apiEndpoint = apiEndpoint.trim();
    device.updatedAt = new Date();

    await device.save();

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Device iot berhasil diupdate',
      data: device
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Terjadi kesalahan ketika mengupdate device iot',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
});

// DELETE IoT device (admin only)
router.delete('/:id', authMiddleware, adminMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const device = await IoTDevice.findById(req.params.id);

    if (!device) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Device IoT tidak ditemukan'
      });
    }

    const Room = require('../models/Room');
    const roomCount = await Room.countDocuments({ iotDeviceId: device._id.toString() });

    if (roomCount > 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Tidak bisa menghapus device yang masih digunakan di room. Silahkan hapus room yang menggunakan device ini terlebih dahulu.',
        roomCount: roomCount
      });
    }

    await device.deleteOne();

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Device iot berhasil dihapus',
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Terjadi kesalahan ketika menghapus device iot',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
});

// PATCH update device status
router.patch('/:id/status', authMiddleware, adminMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { status, lastUpdate } = req.body;

    const device = await IoTDevice.findById(req.params.id);

    if (!device) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Device IoT tidak ditemukan'
      });
    }

    const validStatuses = ['online', 'offline'];
    if (status && !validStatuses.includes(status)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Invalid status. Must be online or offline'
      });
    }

    if (status) device.status = status;
    if (lastUpdate) device.lastUpdate = new Date(lastUpdate);
    device.updatedAt = new Date();

    await device.save();

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Status device berhasil diupdate',
      data: device
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Terjadi kesalahan ketika mengupdate status device',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
});
