const express = require('express');
const router = express.Router();
const Room = require('../models/Room');
const Building = require('../models/Building');
const SensorData = require('../models/SensorData');
const IoTDevice = require('../models/IoTDevice');
const { authMiddleware, adminMiddleware } = require('../middleware/authMiddleware');
const {
  getAQICategory,
  aqiToPm25,
  aqiToSO2,
  aqiToO3,
  aqiToNO2,
} = require('../utils/aqiCalculator');

// ============================================================
// Helper: resolve NAMA device IoT dari room.iotDeviceId
// ============================================================
// Catatan: `iotDeviceId` pada Room disimpan sebagai String (bukan ObjectId),
// jadi `populate()` tidak bisa dipakai — kita resolve manual lewat map.
// Hasil (null) berarti ruangan belum dikaitkan dengan device apapun.
async function resolveDeviceName(deviceId) {
  if (!deviceId) return null;
  try {
    const device = await IoTDevice.findById(deviceId).select('name').lean();
    return device?.name ?? null;
  } catch (_) {
    return null;
  }
}

/** Tambahkan field `iotDeviceName` ke object room (non-destructive). */
function withDeviceName(roomObj, deviceNameById) {
  const deviceId = roomObj.iotDeviceId ? roomObj.iotDeviceId.toString() : null;
  roomObj.iotDeviceName = deviceId ? deviceNameById.get(deviceId) ?? null : null;
  return roomObj;
}

const broadcastRoomUpdate = async (io, room, action, oldData = null) => {
  if (io) {
    // ✅ Sertakan nama device agar UI bisa langsung menampilkan nama,
    // bukan menunggu reload.
    const iotDeviceName = await resolveDeviceName(room.iotDeviceId);

    const updateData = {
      action: action,
      room: {
        id: room._id,
        name: room.name,
        buildingId: room.building,
        buildingName: room.buildingName,
        iotDeviceId: room.iotDeviceId,
        iotDeviceName: iotDeviceName,
        currentAQI: room.currentAQI,
        currentData: room.currentData,
        createdAt: room.createdAt,
        updatedAt: room.updatedAt
      },
      oldData: oldData,
      timestamp: new Date()
    };

    io.to(room._id.toString()).emit('room-updated', updateData);
    io.emit('dashboard-room-updated', updateData);

    if (oldData) {
      if (oldData.name !== room.name) {
        console.log(`🔄 Broadcasting room name change: ${oldData.name} -> ${room.name}`);

        io.emit('room-name-changed', {
          roomId: room._id,
          oldName: oldData.name,
          newName: room.name,
          buildingName: room.buildingName,
          timestamp: new Date()
        });

        io.to(room._id.toString()).emit('room-name-updated', {
          roomId: room._id,
          oldName: oldData.name,
          newName: room.name,
          buildingName: room.buildingName,
          timestamp: new Date()
        });
      }

      if (oldData.buildingId !== room.building.toString()) {
        io.emit('room-building-changed', {
          roomId: room._id,
          oldBuildingId: oldData.buildingId,
          newBuildingId: room.building,
          oldBuildingName: oldData.buildingName,
          newBuildingName: room.buildingName,
          timestamp: new Date()
        });
      }
    }

    console.log(`📢 Broadcast room ${action}: ${room.name} (${room._id})`);
  }
};

// GET all rooms
// ✅ FIXED: sertakan `iotDeviceName` agar halaman Kelola Ruangan bisa
// menampilkan NAMA device yang dikaitkan (bukan label generik "Device IoT").
// Hanya 2 query paralel, jadi tidak menambah latency berarti.
router.get('/', async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const [rooms, devices] = await Promise.all([
      Room.find()
        .populate('building', 'name code')
        .sort({ name: 1 }),
      IoTDevice.find({}, 'name'),
    ]);

    const deviceNameById = new Map(
      devices.map((device) => [device._id.toString(), device.name])
    );

    const data = rooms.map((room) =>
      withDeviceName(room.toObject(), deviceNameById)
    );

    res.json({
      success: true,
      count: data.length,
      data
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Error fetching rooms',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
});

// ============================================================
// DEMO ENDPOINTS — untuk sidang/demo agar dosen bisa melihat
// semua kategori AQI (termasuk "Tidak Sehat" s/d "Berbahaya")
// secara deterministik tanpa menunggu random.
// ============================================================

// POST /demo-cycle — set beberapa ruangan ke kategori berbeda sekaligus.
// Berguna untuk menunjukkan semua warna AQI dalam 1 dashboard.
router.post('/demo-cycle', authMiddleware, adminMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const targets = [
      { aqi: 25,  label: 'Baik' },
      { aqi: 75,  label: 'Sedang' },
      { aqi: 150, label: 'Tidak Sehat' },
      { aqi: 250, label: 'Sangat Tidak Sehat' },
      { aqi: 400, label: 'Berbahaya' },
    ];

    const rooms = await Room.find().sort({ name: 1 }).limit(targets.length);

    if (rooms.length === 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Belum ada ruangan. Tambahkan ruangan terlebih dahulu.'
      });
    }

    const io = req.app.get('socketio');
    const results = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < rooms.length && i < targets.length; i++) {
      const target = targets[i];
      const room = rooms[i];

       const pm25 = aqiToPm25(target.aqi);
      const pm10 = Math.round(pm25 * 1.5);
      const co = target.aqi > 200 ? 20000 : (target.aqi > 100 ? 12000 : 2000);
      const so2 = aqiToSO2(target.aqi);
      const o3 = aqiToO3(target.aqi);
      const no2 = aqiToNO2(target.aqi);
      const temperature = target.aqi > 200 ? 33 : (target.aqi > 100 ? 30 : 26);
      const humidity = target.aqi > 200 ? 80 : (target.aqi > 100 ? 70 : 55);

      room.demoMode = true;
      room.currentAQI = target.aqi;
      room.currentData = {
        pm25, pm10, co, so2, o3, no2,
        temperature, humidity,
        updatedAt: new Date()
      };
      room.updatedAt = new Date();
      await room.save();

      await new SensorData({
        roomId: room._id,
        roomName: room.name,
        buildingName: room.buildingName,
        aqi: target.aqi,
        pm25, pm10, co, so2, o3, no2,
        temperature, humidity,
        category: getAQICategory(target.aqi).category,
        timestamp: new Date(),
      }).save();

      if (io) {
        io.emit('dashboard-update', {
          type: 'room-data-updated',
          roomId: room._id,
          aqi: target.aqi,
          building: room.buildingName,
          timestamp: new Date()
        });
      }

      results.push({
        roomId: room._id,
        room: room.name,
        building: room.buildingName,
        aqi: target.aqi,
        category: getAQICategory(target.aqi).category,
        label: target.label,
      });
    }

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: `Demo cycle diterapkan ke ${results.length} ruangan`,
      results
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Demo cycle error:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: error.message });
  }
});

// POST /demo-aqi — set 1 ruangan ke AQI tertentu (persist sampai reset)
router.post('/:id/demo-aqi', authMiddleware, adminMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { targetAqi } = req.body;
    const target = parseInt(targetAqi, 10);

    if (isNaN(target) || target < 0 || target > 500) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'targetAqi harus bilangan 0-500'
      });
    }

    const room = await Room.findById(req.params.id);
    if (!room) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'Room not found' });
    }

        const pm25 = aqiToPm25(target);
    const pm10 = Math.round(pm25 * 1.5);
    const co = target > 200 ? 20000 : (target > 100 ? 12000 : 2000);
    const so2 = aqiToSO2(target);
    const o3 = aqiToO3(target);
    const no2 = aqiToNO2(target);
    const temperature = target > 200 ? 33 : (target > 100 ? 30 : 26);
    const humidity = target > 200 ? 80 : (target > 100 ? 70 : 55);

    room.demoMode = true;
    room.currentAQI = target;
    room.currentData = {
      pm25, pm10, co, so2, o3, no2,
      temperature, humidity,
      updatedAt: new Date()
    };
    room.updatedAt = new Date();
    await room.save();

    await new SensorData({
      roomId: room._id,
      roomName: room.name,
      buildingName: room.buildingName,
      aqi: target,
      pm25, pm10, co, so2, o3, no2,
      temperature, humidity,
      category: getAQICategory(target).category,
      timestamp: new Date(),
    }).save();

    const io = req.app.get('socketio');
    if (io) {
      io.emit('dashboard-update', {
        type: 'room-data-updated',
        roomId: room._id,
        aqi: target,
        building: room.buildingName,
        timestamp: new Date()
      });
    }

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: `Demo AQI diset ke ${target} untuk ${room.name}`,
      data: {
        roomId: room._id,
        roomName: room.name,
        aqi: target,
        category: getAQICategory(target).category,
        pm25, pm10, co, temperature, humidity
      }
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Demo AQI error:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: error.message });
  }
});

      success: true,
      message: `Demo mode untuk ${room.name} dimatikan. Data akan update otomatis.`,
    });

  }
});

// POST create room (admin only)
router.post('/', authMiddleware, adminMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { name, buildingId, iotDeviceId } = req.body;
    const io = req.app.get('socketio');

    if (!name) {
      return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: 'Room name is required' });
    }

    if (!buildingId) {
      return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: 'Building ID is required' });
    }

    const building = await Building.findById(buildingId);
    if (!building) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'Building not found' });
    }

    if (!iotDeviceId) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'IoT Device ID is required'
      });
    }

    const existingRoom = await Room.findOne({ name: name.trim(), building: buildingId });
    if (existingRoom) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Nama ruangan sudah ada di gedung ini, silahkan ganti nama atau pilih gedung lain'
      });
    }

    const room = new Room({
      name: name.trim(),
      building: buildingId,
      buildingName: building.name,
      iotDeviceId: iotDeviceId,
      currentAQI: null,        // ✅ no_data sampai poll pertama
      currentData: {
        pm25: null, pm10: null, co: null,
        so2: null, o3: null, no2: null,
        temperature: null, humidity: null,
        updatedAt: new Date()
      },
      createdAt: new Date(),
      updatedAt: new Date()
    });

    await room.save();

    building.roomCount = await Room.countDocuments({ building: buildingId });
    await building.save();

    if (io) broadcastRoomUpdate(io, room, 'created');

    res.status(201).json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Room created successfully',
      data: room
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Error creating room',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
});

// PUT update room (admin only)
router.put('/:id', authMiddleware, adminMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { name, buildingId, iotDeviceId } = req.body;
    const io = req.app.get('socketio');

    const room = await Room.findById(req.params.id).populate('building', 'name');
    if (!room) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'Room not found' });
    }

    const oldData = {
      name: room.name,
      buildingId: room.building._id ? room.building._id.toString() : room.building.toString(),
      buildingName: room.buildingName,
      iotDeviceId: room.iotDeviceId || null
    };

    console.log(`🔄 Room update request for: ${room.name}`);

    if (iotDeviceId !== undefined && !iotDeviceId) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'IoT Device ID is required'
      });
    }

    let buildingChanged = false;
    let oldBuildingId = null;
    let newBuilding = null;

    if (buildingId && buildingId !== room.building.toString()) {
      newBuilding = await Building.findById(buildingId);
      if (!newBuilding) {
        return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'Building not found' });
      }

      const existingRoom = await Room.findOne({
        name: name || room.name,
        building: buildingId,
        _id: { $ne: room._id }
      });

      if (existingRoom) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Room name already exists in the new building'
        });
      }

      oldBuildingId = room.building;
      room.building = buildingId;
      room.buildingName = newBuilding.name;
      buildingChanged = true;
    } else if (name && name !== room.name) {
      const existingRoom = await Room.findOne({
        name: name,
        building: room.building,
        _id: { $ne: room._id }
      });

      if (existingRoom) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Nama ruangan sudah ada di gedung ini, silahkan ganti nama atau pilih gedung lain'
        });
      }
    }

    if (name) {
      room.name = name.trim();
    }

    const prevIotDeviceId = oldData.iotDeviceId || null;

    if (iotDeviceId !== undefined) {
      room.iotDeviceId = iotDeviceId;
    }
    room.updatedAt = new Date();

    const deviceChanged = prevIotDeviceId !== (room.iotDeviceId || null);

    if (deviceChanged) {
      room.currentAQI = null;
      room.currentData = {
        pm25: null, pm10: null, co: null,
        so2: null, o3: null, no2: null,
        temperature: null, humidity: null,
        updatedAt: new Date()
      };
      console.log(
        `🔄 Room "${room.name}" device changed (${prevIotDeviceId} → ${room.iotDeviceId}). Data direset.`
      );
    }

    if (!buildingChanged) {
      const currentBuilding = await Building.findById(room.building);
      if (currentBuilding && room.buildingName !== currentBuilding.name) {
        room.buildingName = currentBuilding.name;
      }
    }

    await room.save();

    let deviceFetchStatus = null;

    if (deviceChanged && room.iotDeviceId) {
      try {
        const iotService = require('../services/iotService');
        const fetchResult = await iotService.forceUpdateRoom(room._id.toString());

        if (fetchResult.success) {
          deviceFetchStatus = 'success';

          const refreshed = await Room.findById(room._id).populate('building', 'name');
          if (refreshed) {
            room.currentAQI = refreshed.currentAQI;
            room.currentData = refreshed.currentData;
            room.updatedAt = refreshed.updatedAt;
          }

          console.log(`✅ Device baru langsung fetch: AQI ${room.currentAQI}`);
        } else {
          deviceFetchStatus = 'failed';
          console.warn(
            `⚠️  Force-fetch device baru gagal: ${fetchResult.message}. Data akan diupdate pada poll berikutnya.`
          );
        }
      } catch (iotError) {
        deviceFetchStatus = 'error';
        console.error('⚠️  Error force-fetch device baru:', iotError.message);
      }
    }

    if (buildingChanged) {
      if (oldBuildingId) {
        const oldBuilding = await Building.findById(oldBuildingId);
        if (oldBuilding) {
          oldBuilding.roomCount = await Room.countDocuments({ building: oldBuildingId });
          await oldBuilding.save();

          if (io) {
            io.emit('building-updated', {
              action: 'room-removed',
              buildingId: oldBuildingId,
              roomId: room._id,
              roomName: room.name,
              timestamp: new Date()
            });
          }
        }
      }

      if (newBuilding) {
        newBuilding.roomCount = await Room.countDocuments({ building: buildingId });
        await newBuilding.save();

        if (io) {
          io.emit('building-updated', {
            action: 'room-added',
            buildingId: buildingId,
            roomId: room._id,
            roomName: room.name,
            timestamp: new Date()
          });
        }
      }
    } else {
      const currentBuilding = await Building.findById(room.building);
      if (currentBuilding) {
        currentBuilding.roomCount = await Room.countDocuments({ building: room.building });
        await currentBuilding.save();
      }
    }

    if (io) {
      broadcastRoomUpdate(io, room, 'updated', oldData);

      if (name && name !== oldData.name) {
        io.emit('room-name-changed-immediate', {
          roomId: room._id,
          oldName: oldData.name,
          newName: room.name,
          buildingName: room.buildingName,
          timestamp: new Date()
        });
      }
    }

    const changes = {
      nameChanged: name && name !== oldData.name,
      buildingChanged: buildingChanged,
      deviceChanged: deviceChanged,
      deviceFetchStatus: deviceFetchStatus
    };

    let responseMessage = 'Room updated successfully';
    if (deviceChanged) {
      if (deviceFetchStatus === 'success') {
        responseMessage = 'Room updated & data partikel device baru berhasil dimuat.';
      } else if (deviceFetchStatus === 'failed' || deviceFetchStatus === 'error') {
        responseMessage = 'Room updated. Data partikel akan dimuat pada poll berikutnya karena device baru tidak merespons.';
      } else {
        responseMessage = 'Room updated.';
      }
    }

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: responseMessage,
      data: room,
      changes: changes
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('❌ Error updating room:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Error updating room',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
});

// DELETE room (admin only)
router.delete('/:id', authMiddleware, adminMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const io = req.app.get('socketio');
    const room = await Room.findById(req.params.id);

    if (!room) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'Room not found' });
    }

    const buildingId = room.building;
    const roomData = {
      id: room._id,
      name: room.name,
      buildingName: room.buildingName
    };

    await room.deleteOne();

    const building = await Building.findById(buildingId);
    if (building) {
      building.roomCount = await Room.countDocuments({ building: buildingId });
      await building.save();

      if (io) {
        io.emit('building-updated', {
          action: 'room-deleted',
          buildingId: buildingId,
          room: roomData,
          timestamp: new Date()
        });
      }
    }

    if (io) {
      io.emit('room-deleted', {
        roomId: room._id,
        roomName: room.name,
        buildingName: room.buildingName,
        timestamp: new Date()
      });

      io.to(room._id.toString()).emit('room-updated', {
        action: 'deleted',
        room: roomData,
        timestamp: new Date()
      });
    }

    res.json({ success: true, message: 'Room deleted successfully' });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Error deleting room',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
});

// Force refresh
router.post('/:id/force-refresh', authMiddleware, adminMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const io = req.app.get('socketio');
    const room = await Room.findById(req.params.id);

    if (!room) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'Room not found' });
    }

    if (io) {
      io.emit('room-force-refresh', {
        roomId: room._id,
        roomName: room.name,
        timestamp: new Date()
      });

      io.to(room._id.toString()).emit('room-refresh-requested', {
        timestamp: new Date()
      });
    }

    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Force refresh broadcasted',
      roomId: room._id,
      roomName: room.name
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Error forcing refresh',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
});

// Sync building name
router.post('/:id/sync-building', authMiddleware, adminMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const io = req.app.get('socketio');
    const room = await Room.findById(req.params.id).populate('building', 'name');

    if (!room) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'Room not found' });
    }

    const oldBuildingName = room.buildingName;
    const newBuildingName = room.building.name;

    if (oldBuildingName !== newBuildingName) {
      room.buildingName = newBuildingName;
      room.updatedAt = new Date();
      await room.save();

      if (io) {
        io.emit('room-building-synced', {
          roomId: room._id,
          oldBuildingName: oldBuildingName,
          newBuildingName: newBuildingName,
          timestamp: new Date()
        });
      }

      res.json({
        success: true,
        message: 'Building name synced',
        oldBuildingName,
        newBuildingName
      });
    } else {
      res.json({
        success: true,
        message: 'Building name already synced',
        buildingName: newBuildingName
      });
    }
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Error syncing building name',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
});

module.exports = router;

C:\Users\fatha\Develop\UNILA-AQI\unila-aqi-backend\src\routes\sensorDataRoutes.js

const express = require('express');
const router = express.Router();
const mongoose = require('mongoose');
const SensorData = require('../models/SensorData');

// Helper function untuk convert string ke ObjectId
const toObjectId = (id) => {
  try {
    return new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(id);
  } catch (error) {
    return null;
  }
};

// ============================================================
// Helper: Format tanggal WIB
// ============================================================
const WIB_OFFSET_MS = 7 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

/** Kembalikan tanggal hari ini (WIB) dalam format YYYY-MM-DD */
function getTodayWIBDateStr() {
  const now = new Date();
  const wib = new Date(now.getTime() + WIB_OFFSET_MS);
  return wib.toISOString().split('T')[0];
}

/** Format timestamp menjadi "HH:MM" dalam zona WIB */
function formatWIBTimeLabel(timestamp) {
  const wibDate = new Date(new Date(timestamp).getTime() + WIB_OFFSET_MS);
  const hh = wibDate.getUTCHours().toString().padStart(2, '0');
  const mm = wibDate.getUTCMinutes().toString().padStart(2, '0');
  return `${hh}:${mm}`;
}

// ============================================================
// GET sensor data for a room (24 jam terakhir)
// ============================================================
router.get('/:roomId', async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { roomId } = req.params;

    if (!mongoose.Types.ObjectId.isValid(roomId)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Invalid room ID format'
      });
    }

    // Ambil data 24 jam terakhir
    const now = new Date();
    const dateFilter = { timestamp: { $gte: new Date(now - 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000) } };

    const sensorData = await SensorData.find({
      roomId: toObjectId(roomId),
      ...dateFilter
    }).sort({ timestamp: 1 });

    res.json({
      success: true,
      data: sensorData,
      count: sensorData.length,
      message: '24 hours data loaded'
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error fetching sensor data:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Error fetching sensor data',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
});

// ============================================================
// GET sensor data by date untuk grafik history (WIB aware)
// ============================================================
// FIXED (2026-11):
//   - Query rentang tanggal menggunakan zona WIB (+07:00)
//     sehingga pukul 00:00 WIB s/d 23:59 WIB terambil penuh.
//   - $hour & $minute diberi timezone "+07:00" agar label
//     chart menunjukkan jam WIB, bukan UTC.
//   - Fallback raw data juga diformat ke WIB (bukan UTC).
//   - ✅ BARU: timestamp hasil group di-rebuild via $dateFromParts
//     sehingga SELALU jatuh tepat di menit :00 atau :30 (detik = 0).
//     Ini mencegah label aneh seperti 16:46 di chart frontend.
//
// Pipeline:
//   1. $match      — filter roomId + rentang tanggal WIB
//   2. $addFields  — hitung slot interval (WIB)
//   3. $sort       — urutkan ascending (WAJIB untuk $first)
//   4. $group      — per (hour, interval), ambil $first
//   5. $sort       — urutkan hasil akhir kronologis
//   6. $project    — rebuild timestamp ke slot (detik = 0),
//                    format timeLabel "HH:MM" (WIB)
// ============================================================
router.get('/:roomId/history', async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { roomId } = req.params;
    const { date, interval = 30 } = req.query;

    if (!mongoose.Types.ObjectId.isValid(roomId)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Invalid room ID format'
      });
    }

    // ✅ FIX: Waktu WIB (UTC+7)
    let startDate, endDate;
    if (date) {
      startDate = new Date(`${date}T00:00:00.000+07:00`);
      endDate = new Date(`${date}T23:59:59.999+07:00`);
    } else {
      const wibDate = getTodayWIBDateStr();
      startDate = new Date(`${wibDate}T00:00:00.000+07:00`);
      endDate = new Date(`${wibDate}T23:59:59.999+07:00`);
    }

    const intervalNum = parseInt(interval, 10);
    if (isNaN(intervalNum) || intervalNum <= 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Parameter interval tidak valid',
      });
    }

    console.log(
      `📊 Fetching history data for room ${roomId} from ${startDate.toISOString()} to ${endDate.toISOString()} (interval: ${intervalNum}m)`
    );

    const aggregationPipeline = [
      {
        $match: {
          roomId: toObjectId(roomId),
          timestamp: { $gte: startDate, $lte: endDate }
        }
      },
      {
        $addFields: {
          // ✅ FIX: timezone "+07:00" agar $minute ikut WIB
          timeInterval: {
            $subtract: [
              { $minute: { date: '$timestamp', timezone: '+07:00' } },
              {
                $mod: [
                  { $minute: { date: '$timestamp', timezone: '+07:00' } },
                  intervalNum
                ]
              }
            ]
          }
        }
      },
      {
        $sort: { timestamp: 1 }
      },
      {
        $group: {
          _id: {
            hour: { $hour: { date: '$timestamp', timezone: '+07:00' } },
            interval: '$timeInterval'
          },
          timestamp: { $first: '$timestamp' },
          aqi: { $first: '$aqi' },
          pm25: { $first: '$pm25' },
          pm10: { $first: '$pm10' },
          co: { $first: '$co' },
          so2: { $first: '$so2' },
          o3: { $first: '$o3' },
          no2: { $first: '$no2' },
          temperature: { $first: '$temperature' },
          humidity: { $first: '$humidity' },
          category: { $first: '$category' },
          rawData: { $push: '$$ROOT' }
        }
      },
      {
        $sort: { timestamp: 1 }
      },
      {
        $project: {
          _id: 0,
          // ✅ FIX: rebuild timestamp dari slot (hour + interval, detik = 0)
          // sehingga selalu jatuh tepat di menit :00 atau :30.
          // Frontend history_chart.dart menggunakan field ini untuk
          // menampilkan label jam di sumbu-X.
          timestamp: {
            $dateFromParts: {
              year:   { $year:   { date: '$timestamp', timezone: '+07:00' } },
              month:  { $month:  { date: '$timestamp', timezone: '+07:00' } },
              day:    { $dayOfMonth: { date: '$timestamp', timezone: '+07:00' } },
              hour:   '$_id.hour',
              minute: '$_id.interval',
              second: 0,
              millisecond: 0,
              timezone: '+07:00',
            },
          },
          hour: '$_id.hour',
          interval: '$_id.interval',
          timeLabel: {
            $concat: [
              {
                $cond: {
                  if: { $lt: ['$_id.hour', 10] },
                  then: { $concat: ['0', { $toString: '$_id.hour' }] },
                  else: { $toString: '$_id.hour' },
                },
              },
              ':',
              {
                $cond: {
                  if: { $lt: ['$_id.interval', 10] },
                  then: { $concat: ['0', { $toString: '$_id.interval' }] },
                  else: { $toString: '$_id.interval' },
                },
              },
            ],
          },
          aqi: 1,
          pm25: 1,
          pm10: 1,
          co: 1,
          so2: 1,
          o3: 1,
          no2: 1,
          temperature: 1,
          humidity: 1,
          category: 1,
        }
      }
    ];

    const aggregatedData = await SensorData.aggregate(aggregationPipeline);

    // Fallback: jika tidak ada data agregasi, ambil data mentah
    if (aggregatedData.length === 0) {
      const rawData = await SensorData.find({
        roomId: toObjectId(roomId),
        timestamp: { $gte: startDate, $lte: endDate }
      }).sort({ timestamp: 1 });

      return res.json({
        success: true,
        // ✅ FIX: bulatkan timestamp ke slot 30 menit terdekat (detik = 0)
        // dan format timeLabel ke WIB (bukan getHours() yang UTC).
        data: rawData.map((item) => {
          const obj = item.toObject();

          const wibMs = new Date(item.timestamp).getTime() + WIB_OFFSET_MS;
          const wibDate = new Date(wibMs);
          const roundedMinute = wibDate.getUTCMinutes() < 30 ? 0 : 30;
          const roundedWib = new Date(Date.UTC(
            wibDate.getUTCFullYear(),
            wibDate.getUTCMonth(),
            wibDate.getUTCDate(),
            wibDate.getUTCHours(),
            roundedMinute,
            0,
            0,
          ));

          obj.timestamp = new Date(roundedWib.getTime() - WIB_OFFSET_MS);
          obj.timeLabel = formatWIBTimeLabel(obj.timestamp);
          return obj;
        }),
        aggregated: false,
        count: rawData.length,
        message: 'Raw data loaded'
      });
    }

    res.json({
      success: true,
      data: aggregatedData,
      aggregated: true,
      count: aggregatedData.length,
      startDate,
      endDate,
      message: 'Aggregated history data loaded'
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error fetching history data:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Error fetching history data',
      error: error.message
    });
  }
});

// ============================================================
// GET monthly trend (WIB aware)
// ============================================================
router.get('/:roomId/monthly', async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { roomId } = req.params;
    const year  = parseInt(req.query.year, 10);
    const month = parseInt(req.query.month, 10);

    if (!mongoose.Types.ObjectId.isValid(roomId)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Invalid room ID format',
      });
    }

    if (isNaN(year) || year < 2000 || year > 2100) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Parameter year tidak valid',
      });
    }

    if (isNaN(month) || month < 1 || month > 12) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Parameter month tidak valid (1-12)',
      });
    }

    // ✅ FIX: gunakan zona WIB agar cut-off tanggal akurat
    const startOfMonth = new Date(
      `${year}-${String(month).padStart(2, '0')}-01T00:00:00.000+07:00`
    );
    // Hari terakhir bulan: tanggal 0 dari bulan berikutnya
    const lastDay = new Date(year, month, 0).getDate();
    const endOfMonth = new Date(
      `${year}-${String(month).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(lastDay).padStart(2, '0')}T23:59:59.999+07:00`
    );

    console.log(
      `📊 Monthly trend → room ${roomId} | ${year}-${String(month).padStart(2, '0')} | ` +
      `${startOfMonth.toISOString()} → ${endOfMonth.toISOString()}`
    );

    const allParams = [
      'aqi', 'pm25', 'pm10', 'co', 'so2', 'o3', 'no2',
      'temperature', 'humidity',
    ];

    // Accumulator tahap 1: per tanggal
    const dailyAcc = {};
    allParams.forEach((p) => {
      dailyAcc[`daily_max_${p}`] = { $max: `$${p}` };
      dailyAcc[`daily_avg_${p}`] = { $avg: `$${p}` };
      dailyAcc[`daily_min_${p}`] = { $min: `$${p}` };
    });

    // Accumulator tahap 2: per hari-dalam-minggu
    const monthlyAcc = { weekCount: { $sum: 1 } };
    allParams.forEach((p) => {
      monthlyAcc[`max_${p}`] = { $max: `$daily_max_${p}` };
      monthlyAcc[`avg_${p}`] = { $avg: `$daily_avg_${p}` };
      monthlyAcc[`min_${p}`] = { $min: `$daily_min_${p}` };
    });

    const pipeline = [
      // 1. Filter: room + rentang bulan + BUKAN anomali + BUKAN error (-1)
      {
        $match: {
          roomId: toObjectId(roomId),
          timestamp: { $gte: startOfMonth, $lte: endOfMonth },
          isAnomaly: { $ne: true },
          aqi: { $gte: 0 },
        },
      },
      // 2. Hitung index hari dalam minggu (Senin=1 .. Minggu=7) — WIB
      {
        $addFields: {
          dayOfWeekMonSun: {
            $add: [
              {
                $mod: [
                  {
                    $add: [
                      { $dayOfWeek: { date: '$timestamp', timezone: '+07:00' } },
                      5,
                    ],
                  },
                  7,
                ],
              },
              1,
            ],
          },
        },
      },
      // 3. Group per tanggal (hari kalender WIB)
      {
        $group: {
          _id: {
            day: { $dayOfMonth: { date: '$timestamp', timezone: '+07:00' } },
            dow: '$dayOfWeekMonSun',
          },
          ...dailyAcc,
        },
      },
      // 4. Group per hari-dalam-minggu
      {
        $group: {
          _id: '$_id.dow',
          ...monthlyAcc,
        },
      },
      // 5. Urutkan Senin..Minggu
      { $sort: { _id: 1 } },
    ];

    const rawResult = await SensorData.aggregate(pipeline);

    const dayNames = [
      'Senin', 'Selasa', 'Rabu', 'Kamis', 'Jumat', 'Sabtu', 'Minggu',
    ];

    const round1 = (v) =>
      typeof v === 'number' && !Number.isNaN(v)
        ? Math.round(v * 10) / 10
        : null;

    const days = [];
    for (let i = 1; i <= 7; i++) {
      const found = rawResult.find((r) => r._id === i);

      const values = {};
      allParams.forEach((p) => {
        values[p] = {
          max: found ? round1(found[`max_${p}`]) : null,
          avg: found ? round1(found[`avg_${p}`]) : null,
          min: found ? round1(found[`min_${p}`]) : null,
        };
      });

      const hasData = values.aqi.max !== null;

      days.push({
        day: i,
        dayName: dayNames[i - 1],
        hasData,
        weekCount: found?.weekCount ?? 0,
        values,
      });
    }

    res.json({
      success: true,
      year,
      month,
      roomId,
      startDate: startOfMonth,
      endDate: endOfMonth,
      days,
      message: 'Monthly trend data loaded',
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error fetching monthly trend:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Error fetching monthly trend',
      error: error.message,
    });
  }
});

module.exports = router;

C:\Users\fatha\Develop\UNILA-AQI\unila-aqi-backend\src\services\iotService.js

const axios = require('axios');
const cron = require('node-cron');
const Room = require('../models/Room');
const IoTDevice = require('../models/IoTDevice');
const SensorData = require('../models/SensorData');
const { getAQICategory, calculateAQI } = require('../utils/aqiCalculator');
const { runExclusive } = require('../utils/serviceLock');

// Helper border — dipakai konsisten di semua log blok
const BORDER_TOP = '┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────';
const BORDER_BOTTOM = '└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────';

const LOG_TIME_ZONE = 'Asia/Jakarta';
const LOG_TIME_FORMATTER = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', {
  timeZone: LOG_TIME_ZONE,
  hour: '2-digit',
  minute: '2-digit',
  second: '2-digit',
  hour12: false,
});

/** "19:47:45" dalam WIB */
function formatLogTime(date = new Date()) {
  const parts = LOG_TIME_FORMATTER.formatToParts(date);
  const pick = (type) => parts.find((p) => p.type === type)?.value ?? '00';
  return `${pick('hour')}:${pick('minute')}:${pick('second')}`;
}

/** "Senin, 18 November 2026" dalam WIB */
function formatLogDate(date = new Date()) {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('id-ID', {
    timeZone: LOG_TIME_ZONE,
    weekday: 'long',
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'long',
    year: 'numeric',
  }).format(date);
}

/** "19:47:45 WIB (Senin, 18 November 2026)" */
function formatLogDateTime(date = new Date()) {
  return `${formatLogTime(date)} WIB (${formatLogDate(date)})`;
}

class IoTService {
  constructor() {
    this.isRunning = false;
    this.task = null;
    this.io = null;
    this.deviceLastData = new Map();
    this.pollingInterval = 30000;
    this._polling = false;        // ✅ guard anti-tumpang-tindih siklus cron
    this._maxRetries = 2;         // ✅ retry untuk device lambat / 503
    this._retryDelayMs = 3000;
  }

  start(io) {
    if (this.isRunning) {
      console.log('⚠️ IoT Service is already running');
      return;
    }

    this.io = io;
    console.log(`🚀 Starting IoT Service (polling every ${this.pollingInterval / 1000}s)...`);

    // IoT berjalan di detik ke-15 dan ke-45 setiap menit.
    this.task = cron.schedule('15,45 * * * * *', async () => {
      // ✅ Guard: jangan mulai siklus baru jika siklus sebelumnya belum selesai
      if (this._polling) {
        console.log('⏭️  Skipping poll — previous cycle still running');
        return;
      }

      this._polling = true;
      try {
        await this.pollAllIoTDevices();
      } finally {
        this._polling = false;
      }
    });

    this.isRunning = true;
    console.log('✅ IoT Service started');
    this.pollAllIoTDevices();
  }

  stop() {
    if (this.task) {
      this.task.stop();
      this.isRunning = false;
      console.log('⏹️ IoT Service stopped');
    }
  }

  /**
   * Helper: GET dengan retry sederhana untuk device yang lambat / 503.
   */
  async _getWithRetry(url, timeoutMs = 15000) {
    let lastError;
    for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= this._maxRetries; attempt++) {
      try {
        return await axios.get(url, { timeout: timeoutMs });
      } catch (err) {
        lastError = err;
        if (attempt < this._maxRetries) {
          await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, this._retryDelayMs));
        }
      }
    }
    throw lastError;
  }

  async pollAllIoTDevices() {
    return runExclusive(async () => {
      try {
        const devices = await IoTDevice.find({});

        if (devices.length === 0) {
          console.log('ℹ️ No IoT devices to poll');
          return;
        }

        console.log(`🔄 Polling ${devices.length} IoT devices...`);

        // ✅ Cetak SERIAL di sini (bukan di dalam pollDevice yang paralel)
        devices.forEach((d) => {
          console.log(`📡 Polling device: ${d.name} (${d.apiEndpoint})`);
        });

        // Poll paralel, tapi TIDAK mencetak log di dalam pollDevice.
        const results = await Promise.allSettled(
          devices.map(async (device) => {
            try {
              const res = await this.pollDevice(device);
              return { ...res, deviceName: device.name };
            } catch (error) {
              return { success: false, deviceName: device.name, error: error.message };
            }
          })
        );

        // ✅ Log SERIAL — semua pakai console.log agar satu stream (stdout),
        // sehingga urutan border + isi dijamin konsisten.
        let successfulPolls = 0;
        for (const r of results) {
          const val =
            r.status === 'fulfilled'
              ? r.value
              : { success: false, deviceName: 'unknown', error: r.reason?.message };

          console.log(BORDER_TOP);
          if (val && val.success === true) {
            successfulPolls++;
            const lines = val.roomLogs || [];
            if (lines.length === 0) {
              console.log(
                `ℹ️  Device ${val.deviceName}: no rooms updated (no rooms mapped or all demoMode)`
              );
            } else {
              lines.forEach((line) => console.log(line));
            }
          } else {
            // ✅ console.log (bukan console.error) — hindari urutan acak stdout/stderr
            const name = val?.deviceName || 'unknown';
            const errMsg = val?.error || 'unknown error';
            console.log(`❌ Error polling device ${name}: ${errMsg}`);
          }
          console.log(BORDER_BOTTOM);
        }

        console.log(`\n✅ IoT polling completed: ${successfulPolls}/${devices.length} successful`);
        console.log(`🕒 Updated at ${formatLogDateTime()} WIB`);
        console.log('\n=====================================================================================\n');
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('❌ Error in pollAllIoTDevices:', error.message);
      }
    });
  }

  async pollDevice(device) {
    try {
      // ✅ TIDAK ada console.log di sini — dicetak serial oleh pollAllIoTDevices
      const response = await this._getWithRetry(device.apiEndpoint, 15000);

      if (response.data?.success === true) {
        const iotData = response.data.data;

        if (!this.isValidIoTData(iotData)) {
          throw new Error('Invalid IoT data structure');
        }

        const lastData = this.deviceLastData.get(device._id.toString());
        const hasChanged = this._hasDataChanged(lastData, iotData);

        this.deviceLastData.set(device._id.toString(), iotData);

        device.status = 'online';
        device.lastUpdate = new Date();
        await device.save();

        const rooms = await Room.find({
          iotDeviceId: device._id.toString(),
        });

        const roomLogs = [];
        for (const room of rooms) {
          const { logs } = await this.updateRoomFromIoT(room, iotData, device.name);
          if (logs && logs.length) roomLogs.push(...logs);
        }

        return { success: true, changed: hasChanged, roomLogs };
      }

      return {
        success: false,
        deviceName: device.name,
        error: 'Device did not return success',
      };
    } catch (error) {
      device.status = 'offline';
      await device.save().catch(() => {});
      return {
        success: false,
        deviceName: device.name,
        error: error.message,
      };
    }
  }

  async checkDeviceStatus(devices) {
    if (!devices || devices.length === 0) return [];

    return runExclusive(async () => {
      const results = await Promise.allSettled(
        devices.map(async (device) => {
          try {
            await this._getWithRetry(device.apiEndpoint, 3000);
            return { id: device._id.toString(), status: 'online' };
          } catch (_) {
            return { id: device._id.toString(), status: 'offline' };
          }
        })
      );

      const updates = results
        .filter((r) => r.status === 'fulfilled' && r.value)
        .map((r) => r.value);

      if (updates.length > 0) {
        const bulkOps = updates.map((u) => ({
          updateOne: {
            filter: { _id: u.id },
            update: { $set: { status: u.status } },
          },
        }));

        try {
          await IoTDevice.bulkWrite(bulkOps);
        } catch (bulkErr) {
          console.error('❌ bulkWrite status check failed:', bulkErr.message);
        }
      }

      return updates;
    });
  }

  isValidIoTData(data) {
    if (!data || typeof data !== 'object') return false;

    const knownFields = [
      'aqi', 'pm25', 'pm10', 'co', 'so2', 'o3', 'no2',
      'temperature', 'humidity',
    ];
    return knownFields.some((k) => k in data);
  }

  _hasDataChanged(oldData, newData) {
    if (!oldData || !newData) return true;

    const changed = (oldV, newV) => {
      if (oldV == null && newV == null) return false;
      if (oldV == null || newV == null) return true;
      return Math.abs(Number(newV) - Number(oldV)) > 0;
    };

    return (
      changed(oldData.aqi, newData.aqi) ||
      changed(oldData.pm25, newData.pm25) ||
      changed(oldData.pm10, newData.pm10) ||
      changed(oldData.co, newData.co) ||
      changed(oldData.so2, newData.so2) ||
      changed(oldData.o3, newData.o3) ||
      changed(oldData.no2, newData.no2) ||
      changed(oldData.temperature, newData.temperature) ||
      changed(oldData.humidity, newData.humidity)
    );
  }

  _dominantLabel(aqiResult) {
    if (!aqiResult || !aqiResult.dominant) return null;
    return (
      {
        pm25: `PM2.5 (ISPU=${aqiResult.ispuPM25})`,
        pm10: `PM10 (ISPU=${aqiResult.ispuPM10})`,
        co:   `CO (ISPU=${aqiResult.ispuCO})`,
        so2:  `SO2 (ISPU=${aqiResult.ispuSO2})`,
        o3:   `O3 (ISPU=${aqiResult.ispuO3})`,
        no2:  `NO2 (ISPU=${aqiResult.ispuNO2})`,
      }[aqiResult.dominant] || aqiResult.dominant
    );
  }

  async updateRoomFromIoT(room, iotData, deviceName) {
    const logs = [];
    try {
      if (room.demoMode) {
        logs.push(`ℹ️  Skipping room ${room.name} (demoMode active)`);
        return { logs };
      }

      const optNum = (v) =>
        typeof v === 'number' && Number.isFinite(v) && !Number.isNaN(v) ? v : null;

      const pm25 = optNum(iotData.pm25);
      const pm10 = optNum(iotData.pm10);
      const co   = optNum(iotData.co);
      const so2  = optNum(iotData.so2);
      const o3   = optNum(iotData.o3);
      const no2  = optNum(iotData.no2);
      const temperature = optNum(iotData.temperature);
      const humidity    = optNum(iotData.humidity);

      const hasError = [pm25, pm10, co, so2, o3, no2, temperature, humidity]
        .some((v) => v !== null && v < 0);

      if (hasError) {
        await this.handleDeviceError(room, deviceName, logs);
        return { logs };
      }

      const aqiResult = calculateAQI(pm25, pm10, co, so2, o3, no2);

      const normalizedData = {
        aqi: aqiResult.aqi,
        pm25, pm10, co, so2, o3, no2,
        temperature, humidity,
        category: aqiResult.category,
        dominant: aqiResult.dominant,
      };

      const currentData = room.currentData;
      const hasRoomDataChanged = this._hasDataChanged(
        {
          aqi: room.currentAQI,
          pm25: currentData.pm25,
          pm10: currentData.pm10,
          co: currentData.co,
          so2: currentData.so2,
          o3: currentData.o3,
          no2: currentData.no2,
          temperature: currentData.temperature,
          humidity: currentData.humidity,
        },
        normalizedData
      );

      if (!hasRoomDataChanged) {
        logs.push(`ℹ️  Room ${room.name} data unchanged (exact match), skipping room update`);
      } else {
        room.currentAQI = normalizedData.aqi;
        room.currentData = {
          pm25, pm10, co, so2, o3, no2,
          temperature, humidity,
          updatedAt: new Date(),
        };
        room.updatedAt = new Date();
        await room.save();

        this.broadcastRoomUpdate(room, normalizedData, 'iot', deviceName);
        logs.push(`📢 Broadcast IoT update for room ${room.name}: AQI ${room.currentAQI}`);
      }

      await new SensorData({
        roomId: room._id,
        roomName: room.name,
        buildingName: room.buildingName,
        aqi: normalizedData.aqi,
        pm25, pm10, co, so2, o3, no2,
        temperature, humidity,
        category: aqiResult.category,
        timestamp: new Date(),
        isAnomaly: iotData.isAnomaly === true,
        anomalyType: iotData.anomalyType || null,
      }).save();

      if (normalizedData.aqi == null) {
        logs.push(
          `ℹ️  IoT room ${room.name} (device ${deviceName}): tidak ada data valid — status no_data`
        );
      } else if (normalizedData.aqi > 100) {
        logs.push(
          `🚨 HIGH AQI in ${room.name} (IoT ${deviceName}): ${normalizedData.aqi} (${aqiResult.category})`
        );
      } else {
        logs.push(
          `✅ IoT room ${room.name}: AQI ${normalizedData.aqi}, ` +
            `PM2.5=${pm25}, PM10=${pm10}, ` +
            `CO=${co}, Suhu=${temperature}, RH=${humidity}`
        );
      }

      if (aqiResult.dominant) {
        const dominantLabel = this._dominantLabel(aqiResult);
        logs.push(
          `⚠️  DOMINAN ${aqiResult.dominant.toUpperCase()} in ${room.name}: AQI ${aqiResult.aqi} (${dominantLabel})`
        );
      }

      return { logs };
    } catch (error) {
      logs.push(`❌ Error updating IoT room ${room.name}: ${error.message}`);
      return { logs };
    }
  }

  async updateRoomFromIoTForce(room, iotData, deviceName) {
    const logs = [];
    try {
      if (room.demoMode) {
        logs.push(`ℹ️  Skipping room ${room.name} (demoMode active)`);
        return { logs };
      }

      const optNum = (v) =>
        typeof v === 'number' && Number.isFinite(v) && !Number.isNaN(v) ? v : null;

      const pm25 = optNum(iotData.pm25);
      const pm10 = optNum(iotData.pm10);
      const co   = optNum(iotData.co);
      const so2  = optNum(iotData.so2);
      const o3   = optNum(iotData.o3);
      const no2  = optNum(iotData.no2);
      const temperature = optNum(iotData.temperature);
      const humidity    = optNum(iotData.humidity);

      const hasError = [pm25, pm10, co, so2, o3, no2, temperature, humidity]
        .some((v) => v !== null && v < 0);

      if (hasError) {
        await this.handleDeviceError(room, deviceName, logs);
        return { logs };
      }

      const aqiResult = calculateAQI(pm25, pm10, co, so2, o3, no2);

      const normalizedData = {
        aqi: aqiResult.aqi,
        pm25, pm10, co, so2, o3, no2,
        temperature, humidity,
        category: aqiResult.category,
        dominant: aqiResult.dominant,
      };

      room.currentAQI = normalizedData.aqi;
      room.currentData = {
        pm25, pm10, co, so2, o3, no2,
        temperature, humidity,
        updatedAt: new Date(),
      };
      room.updatedAt = new Date();
      await room.save();

      this.broadcastRoomUpdate(room, normalizedData, 'iot', deviceName);
      logs.push(`📢 FORCE Broadcast IoT update for room ${room.name}: AQI ${room.currentAQI}`);

      await new SensorData({
        roomId: room._id,
        roomName: room.name,
        buildingName: room.buildingName,
        aqi: normalizedData.aqi,
        pm25, pm10, co, so2, o3, no2,
        temperature, humidity,
        category: aqiResult.category,
        timestamp: new Date(),
        isAnomaly: iotData.isAnomaly === true,
        anomalyType: iotData.anomalyType || null,
      }).save();

      if (normalizedData.aqi == null) {
        logs.push(
          `ℹ️  IoT room ${room.name} (device ${deviceName}): tidak ada data valid — status no_data`
        );
      } else if (normalizedData.aqi > 100) {
        logs.push(
          `🚨 HIGH AQI in ${room.name} (IoT ${deviceName}): ${normalizedData.aqi} (${aqiResult.category})`
        );
      } else {
        logs.push(
          `✅ IoT room ${room.name}: AQI ${normalizedData.aqi}, ` +
            `PM2.5=${pm25}, PM10=${pm10}, ` +
            `CO=${co}, Suhu=${temperature}, RH=${humidity}`
        );
      }

      if (aqiResult.dominant) {
        const dominantLabel = this._dominantLabel(aqiResult);
        logs.push(
          `⚠️  DOMINAN ${aqiResult.dominant.toUpperCase()} in ${room.name}: AQI ${aqiResult.aqi} (${dominantLabel})`
        );
      }

      return { logs };
    } catch (error) {
      logs.push(`❌ Error force-updating IoT room ${room.name}: ${error.message}`);
      return { logs };
    }
  }

  async handleDeviceError(room, deviceName, logs = []) {
    try {
      await new SensorData({
        roomId: room._id,
        roomName: room.name,
        buildingName: room.buildingName,
        aqi: -1,
        pm25: -1, pm10: -1, co: -1, so2: -1, o3: -1, no2: -1,
        temperature: -1, humidity: -1,
        category: 'error',
        timestamp: new Date(),
        isAnomaly: true,
        anomalyType: 'error',
      }).save();

      logs.push(
        `⚠️ IoT SENSOR ERROR in ${room.name} (device: ${deviceName}) — room retained last good value`
      );
    } catch (err) {
      logs.push(`❌ Error handling device error for ${room.name}: ${err.message}`);
    }
  }

  async forceUpdateRoom(roomId) {
    return runExclusive(async () => {
      try {
        const room = await Room.findById(roomId);
        if (!room) return { success: false, message: 'Room not found' };

        if (!room.iotDeviceId) {
          return { success: false, message: 'Room is not configured for IoT' };
        }

        const device = await IoTDevice.findById(room.iotDeviceId);
        if (!device) return { success: false, message: 'IoT device not found' };

        const response = await this._getWithRetry(device.apiEndpoint, 15000);

        if (response.data?.success !== true) {
          return { success: false, message: 'Device did not return success' };
        }

        const iotData = response.data.data;
        if (!this.isValidIoTData(iotData)) {
          return { success: false, message: 'Invalid IoT data structure' };
        }

        device.status = 'online';
        device.lastUpdate = new Date();
        await device.save();

        this.deviceLastData.set(device._id.toString(), iotData);

        const { logs } = await this.updateRoomFromIoTForce(room, iotData, device.name);

        // ✅ Semua console.log (satu stream) — urutan dijamin
        console.log(BORDER_TOP);
        logs.forEach((line) => console.log(line));
        console.log(BORDER_BOTTOM);

        return { success: true, data: iotData, deviceName: device.name };
      } catch (error) {
        console.log(BORDER_TOP);
        console.log(`❌ forceUpdateRoom(${roomId}) error: ${error.message}`);
        console.log(BORDER_BOTTOM);
        return { success: false, error: error.message };
      }
    });
  }

  async pollDeviceForce(device) {
    try {
      const response = await this._getWithRetry(device.apiEndpoint, 15000);

      if (response.data?.success !== true) {
        return { success: false, deviceName: device.name, error: 'Device did not return success' };
      }

      const iotData = response.data.data;
      if (!this.isValidIoTData(iotData)) {
        return { success: false, deviceName: device.name, error: 'Invalid IoT data' };
      }

      this.deviceLastData.set(device._id.toString(), iotData);

      device.status = 'online';
      device.lastUpdate = new Date();
      await device.save();

      const rooms = await Room.find({ iotDeviceId: device._id.toString() });
      const roomLogs = [];
      for (const room of rooms) {
        const { logs } = await this.updateRoomFromIoTForce(room, iotData, device.name);
        if (logs && logs.length) roomLogs.push(...logs);
      }

      return { success: true, changed: true, roomLogs };
    } catch (error) {
      device.status = 'offline';
      await device.save().catch(() => {});
      return { success: false, deviceName: device.name, error: error.message };
    }
  }

  broadcastRoomUpdate(room, data, source, deviceName = null) {
    if (this.io) {
      const updateData = {
        roomId: room._id,
        data: {
          currentAQI: room.currentAQI,
          currentData: room.currentData,
          updatedAt: room.updatedAt,
        },
        timestamp: new Date(),
        source: source,
        deviceName: deviceName,
      };

      this.io.to(room._id.toString()).emit('room-update', updateData);

      this.io.emit('dashboard-update', {
        type: 'room-data-updated',
        roomId: room._id,
        aqi: room.currentAQI,
        building: room.buildingName,
        timestamp: new Date(),
      });
    }
  }

  async forcePollAll() {
    return runExclusive(async () => {
      console.log('🔄 FORCE polling all IoT devices...');
      try {
        const devices = await IoTDevice.find({});
        if (devices.length === 0) {
          return { success: true, message: 'No IoT devices to poll', count: 0 };
        }

        const results = await Promise.allSettled(
          devices.map((d) =>
            this.pollDeviceForce(d).catch((e) => ({
              success: false,
              deviceName: d.name,
              error: e?.message || String(e),
            }))
          )
        );

        let successCount = 0;
        for (const r of results) {
          const val =
            r.status === 'fulfilled'
              ? r.value
              : { success: false, deviceName: 'unknown', error: r.reason?.message };

          console.log(BORDER_TOP);
          if (val && val.success === true) {
            successCount++;
            const lines = val.roomLogs || [];
            if (lines.length === 0) {
              console.log(
                `ℹ️  Device ${val.deviceName || 'unknown'}: no rooms updated (no rooms mapped or all demoMode)`
              );
            } else {
              lines.forEach((line) => console.log(line));
            }
          } else {
            // ✅ console.log (bukan console.error) — satu stream
            const name = val?.deviceName || 'unknown';
            const errMsg = val?.error || 'unknown error';
            console.log(`❌ Force poll failed for ${name}: ${errMsg}`);
          }
          console.log(BORDER_BOTTOM);
        }

        console.log(`✅ FORCE poll completed: ${successCount}/${devices.length}`);
        return {
          success: true,
          message: 'Force poll completed',
          polled: devices.length,
          successful: successCount,
        };
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('❌ forcePollAll error:', error.message);
        return { success: false, error: error.message };
      }
    });
  }

  getStatus() {
    return {
      isRunning: this.isRunning,
      lastUpdate: new Date(),
      nextUpdate: new Date(Date.now() + this.pollingInterval),
      socketConnected: this.io !== null,
      pollingInterval: this.pollingInterval,
      isPolling: this._polling,
    };
  }
}

const iotService = new IoTService();
module.exports = iotService;

C:\Users\fatha\Develop\UNILA-AQI\unila-aqi-backend\src\utils\__tests__\aqiCalculator.test.js

/**
 * ============================================================================
 * UNIT TEST — src/utils/aqiCalculator.js
 * ============================================================================
 *
 * Menguji 4 fungsi publik dari aqiCalculator.js:
 *   1. calculateISPUFromPM25(conc)       → ISPU 0-500 (dari PM2.5)
 *   2. calculateISPUFromPM10(conc)       → ISPU 0-500 (dari PM10)
 *   3. calculateAQI(pm25, pm10, co)      → { aqi, dominant, ispuPM25, ... }
 *   4. getAQICategory(aqi)               → { category, label, color }
 *
 * Standar acuan: PP No. 22 Tahun 2021 (Indonesia).
 *
 * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * RUMUS INTERPOLASI LINEAR
 * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 *   ISPU = ((ISPU_high − ISPU_low) / (C_high − C_low)) × (C − C_low) + ISPU_low
 *
 * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * KATEGORI ISPU (PP 22/2021)
 * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 *   0   –  50   : Baik
 *   51  – 100   : Sedang
 *   101 – 200   : Tidak Sehat
 *   201 – 300   : Sangat Tidak Sehat
 *   301 – 500   : Berbahaya
 *
 * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * POLA PENGUJIAN TIAP FUNGSI
 * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 *   A. NILAI DI TENGAH SEGMEN  → verifikasi rumus interpolasi benar
 *   B. NILAI TEPAT DI BOUNDARY → pastikan tidak "bocor" ke segmen sebelah
 *   C. PERPINDAHAN SEGMEN      → pastikan titik "naik kelas" benar (+1 unit)
 *   D. NILAI EKSTREM / INVALID → pastikan clamp & sanitasi input bekerja
 *
 * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * STRUKTUR FILE
 * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 *   BAGIAN 1 — calculateISPUFromPM25
 *   BAGIAN 2 — calculateISPUFromPM10
 *   BAGIAN 3 — calculateAQI  (MAX dari ketiga parameter)
 *   BAGIAN 4 — getAQICategory (batas antar kategori)
 * ============================================================================
 */

const { test, describe } = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert');

const {
  calculateISPUFromPM25,
  calculateISPUFromPM10,
  calculateAQI,
  getAQICategory,
} = require('../aqiCalculator');


// ============================================================================
// BAGIAN 1 — calculateISPUFromPM25
// ============================================================================
// Tabel breakpoint PM2.5 (µg/m³) → ISPU:
//
//   ISPU          Konsentrasi (µg/m³)
//   ─────         ───────────────────
//     0 –  50        0 –  55
//    51 – 100       56 – 150
//   101 – 200      151 – 250
//   201 – 300      251 – 350
//   301 – 500      351 – 500
// ============================================================================
describe('calculateISPUFromPM25 — konversi PM2.5 (µg/m³) → ISPU', () => {

  // ── A. Nilai di tengah segmen (verifikasi rumus interpolasi) ─────────────

  test('PM2.5 = 0 → ISPU 0 (titik awal, tanpa interpolasi)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(calculateISPUFromPM25(0), 0);
  });

  test('PM2.5 = 27.5 → ISPU 25 (tengah segmen 0–55)', () => {
    // (50 − 0)/(55 − 0) × (27.5 − 0) + 0 = 25
    assert.strictEqual(calculateISPUFromPM25(27.5), 25);
  });

  test('PM2.5 = 100 → ISPU 74 (tengah segmen 56–150)', () => {
    // (100 − 51)/(150 − 56) × (100 − 56) + 51
    //   = (49/94) × 44 + 51
    //   = 22.94 + 51
    //   ≈ 73.93  → dibulatkan 74
    assert.strictEqual(calculateISPUFromPM25(100), 74);
  });

  // ── B. Nilai tepat di boundary (tidak boleh "bocor" ke segmen sebelah) ──

  test('PM2.5 = 55 → ISPU 50 (batas atas segmen Baik, BUKAN 51)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(calculateISPUFromPM25(55), 50);
  });

  test('PM2.5 = 150 → ISPU 100 (batas atas segmen Sedang)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(calculateISPUFromPM25(150), 100);
  });

  test('PM2.5 = 250 → ISPU 200 (batas atas segmen Tidak Sehat)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(calculateISPUFromPM25(250), 200);
  });

  // ── C. Perpindahan segmen (1 unit di atas boundary) ──────────────────────

  test('PM2.5 = 56 → ISPU 51 (naik 1 tingkat ke segmen Sedang)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(calculateISPUFromPM25(56), 51);
  });

  test('PM2.5 = 151 → ISPU 101 (naik 1 tingkat ke segmen Tidak Sehat)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(calculateISPUFromPM25(151), 101);
  });

  // ── D. Nilai ekstrem & input tidak valid (clamp + sanitasi) ──────────────

  test('PM2.5 = −5 (negatif) → ISPU 0 (di-clamp ke batas bawah)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(calculateISPUFromPM25(-5), 0);
  });

  test('PM2.5 = 1000 (di atas 500) → ISPU 500 (di-clamp ke batas atas)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(calculateISPUFromPM25(1000), 500);
  });

  test('PM2.5 = NaN → ISPU 0 (input tidak valid disanitasi)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(calculateISPUFromPM25(NaN), 0);
  });

  test('PM2.5 = null → ISPU 0 (input null disanitasi)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(calculateISPUFromPM25(null), 0);
  });
});


// ============================================================================
// BAGIAN 2 — calculateISPUFromPM10
// ============================================================================
// Tabel breakpoint PM10 (µg/m³) → ISPU:
//
//   ISPU          Konsentrasi (µg/m³)
//   ─────         ───────────────────
//     0 –  50        0 –  50
//    51 – 100       51 – 150
//   101 – 200      151 – 350
//   201 – 300      351 – 420
//   301 – 500      421 – 500
//
// CATATAN: seluruh test di bagian ini fokus pada BOUNDARY karena breakpoint
// PM10 bersinggungan tepat di integer (0/50/51/150/…). Rumus interpolasi
// sudah divalidasi lengkap di BAGIAN 1, jadi di sini cukup memastikan setiap
// batas segmen jatuh di ISPU yang benar.
// ============================================================================
describe('calculateISPUFromPM10 — konversi PM10 (µg/m³) → ISPU', () => {

  test('PM10 = 50 → ISPU 50 (batas atas segmen Baik)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(calculateISPUFromPM10(50), 50);
  });

  test('PM10 = 51 → ISPU 51 (batas bawah segmen Sedang)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(calculateISPUFromPM10(51), 51);
  });

  test('PM10 = 150 → ISPU 100 (batas atas segmen Sedang)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(calculateISPUFromPM10(150), 100);
  });

  test('PM10 = 350 → ISPU 200 (batas atas segmen Tidak Sehat)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(calculateISPUFromPM10(350), 200);
  });

  test('PM10 = 420 → ISPU 300 (batas atas segmen Sangat Tidak Sehat)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(calculateISPUFromPM10(420), 300);
  });
});


// ============================================================================
// BAGIAN 3 — calculateAQI
// ============================================================================
// AQI = MAX(ISPU_PM25, ISPU_PM10, ISPU_CO).
//
// `dominant` = parameter penyumbang nilai MAX.
//
// ATURAN TIE (nilai ISPU sama di beberapa parameter):
//   Urutan pengecekan di implementasi adalah CO → PM2.5 → PM10.
//   Konsekuensi:
//     • CO tie dengan parameter lain   → CO menang
//     • PM2.5 tie dengan PM10          → PM2.5 menang
//
// Skenario yang diuji:
//   1. Masing-masing parameter menjadi dominan (PM10, PM2.5, CO)
//   2. Tie 2 arah & tie 3 arah
//   3. Konsistensi category/label mengikuti nilai MAX
// ============================================================================
describe('calculateAQI — AQI = MAX(ISPU PM2.5, ISPU PM10, ISPU CO)', () => {

  // ── Skenario 1: parameter dominan ────────────────────────────────────────

  test('PM10 dominan: PM2.5=20, PM10=60 → AQI 55, dominant "pm10"', () => {
    // PM2.5: (50/55) × 20                  = 18
    // PM10 : (49/99) × (60−51) + 51        = 55.45 → 55
    // CO   : 0                              = 0
    // MAX(18, 55, 0) = 55 → PM10 dominan
    const r = calculateAQI(20, 60);
    assert.strictEqual(r.aqi, 55);
    assert.strictEqual(r.dominant, 'pm10');
    assert.strictEqual(r.ispuPM25, 18);
    assert.strictEqual(r.ispuPM10, 55);
  });

  test('PM2.5 dominan: PM2.5=100, PM10=30 → AQI 74, dominant "pm25"', () => {
    // PM2.5: (49/94) × (100−56) + 51       = 73.93 → 74
    // PM10 : (50/50) × 30                  = 30
    // CO   : 0                              = 0
    // MAX(74, 30, 0) = 74 → PM2.5 dominan
    const r = calculateAQI(100, 30);
    assert.strictEqual(r.aqi, 74);
    assert.strictEqual(r.dominant, 'pm25');
  });

  test('CO dominan: PM2.5=10, PM10=20, CO=20000 → AQI 234, dominant "co"', () => {
    // PM2.5: (50/55) × 10                  ≈ 9
    // PM10 : (50/50) × 20                  = 20
    // CO   : segmen 15001–30000 µg/m³      → (99/14999) × (20000−15001) + 201 ≈ 234
    // MAX(9, 20, 234) = 234 → CO dominan
    const r = calculateAQI(10, 20, 20000);
    assert.strictEqual(r.aqi, 234);
    assert.strictEqual(r.dominant, 'co');
    assert.ok(r.ispuCO > r.ispuPM25);
    assert.ok(r.ispuCO > r.ispuPM10);
  });

  // ── Skenario 2: tie (nilai ISPU sama antar parameter) ────────────────────

  test('Tie PM2.5 vs PM10, CO rendah → dominant "pm25"', () => {
    // PM2.5 = 30 → ISPU (50/55) × 30 ≈ 27
    // PM10  = 27 → ISPU (50/50) × 27 = 27
    // CO    =  0 → ISPU = 0 (tidak ikut tie)
    // Tie PM2.5 vs PM10 → PM2.5 menang (dicek lebih dulu)
    const r = calculateAQI(30, 27, 0);
    assert.strictEqual(r.ispuPM25, r.ispuPM10);
    assert.ok(r.ispuPM25 > r.ispuCO);
    assert.strictEqual(r.dominant, 'pm25');
  });

  test('Tie 3 arah (semua ISPU = 0) → dominant "co"', () => {
    // PM2.5 = 0, PM10 = 0, CO = 0 → semua ISPU = 0
    // Tie 3 arah → CO menang (dicek lebih dulu)
    const r = calculateAQI(0, 0, 0);
    assert.strictEqual(r.aqi, 0);
    assert.strictEqual(r.ispuPM25, 0);
    assert.strictEqual(r.ispuPM10, 0);
    assert.strictEqual(r.ispuCO, 0);
    assert.strictEqual(r.dominant, 'co');
  });

  // ── Skenario 3: konsistensi category & label ─────────────────────────────

  test('Kategori mengikuti nilai MAX: AQI 55 → category "sedang", label "Sedang"', () => {
    const r = calculateAQI(20, 60);
    assert.strictEqual(r.category, 'sedang');
    assert.strictEqual(r.label, 'Sedang');
  });

  test('Semua input nol → AQI 0, category "baik"', () => {
    const r = calculateAQI(0, 0);
    assert.strictEqual(r.aqi, 0);
    assert.strictEqual(r.category, 'baik');
  });

  test('Input sangat tinggi (600, 600) → AQI 500 (clamped), category "berbahaya"', () => {
    const r = calculateAQI(600, 600);
    assert.strictEqual(r.aqi, 500);
    assert.strictEqual(r.category, 'berbahaya');
  });
});


// ============================================================================
// BAGIAN 4 — getAQICategory
// ============================================================================
// Memastikan nilai AQI jatuh ke kategori yang benar tepat di batas:
//
//   Batas 50/51   : Baik             ↔ Sedang
//   Batas 100/101 : Sedang           ↔ Tidak Sehat
//   Batas 200/201 : Tidak Sehat      ↔ Sangat Tidak Sehat
//   Batas 300/301 : Sangat Tidak Sehat ↔ Berbahaya
//
// Pola pengujian: setiap transisi diuji dengan SEPASANG test — nilai tepat
// di batas atas kategori lama (n), dan n+1 untuk memastikan "naik kelas".
// ============================================================================
describe('getAQICategory — batas kategori sesuai PP 22/2021', () => {

  // ── Baik ↔ Sedang (batas 50/51) ──────────────────────────────────────────

  test('AQI = 50 → category "baik" (batas atas)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(getAQICategory(50).category, 'baik');
  });

  test('AQI = 51 → category "sedang" (batas bawah)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(getAQICategory(51).category, 'sedang');
  });

  // ── Sedang ↔ Tidak Sehat (batas 100/101) ─────────────────────────────────

  test('AQI = 100 → category "sedang" (batas atas)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(getAQICategory(100).category, 'sedang');
  });

  test('AQI = 101 → category "tidak_sehat" (batas bawah)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(getAQICategory(101).category, 'tidak_sehat');
  });

  // ── Tidak Sehat ↔ Sangat Tidak Sehat (batas 200/201) ─────────────────────

  test('AQI = 200 → category "tidak_sehat" (batas atas)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(getAQICategory(200).category, 'tidak_sehat');
  });

  test('AQI = 201 → category "sangat_tidak_sehat" (batas bawah)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(getAQICategory(201).category, 'sangat_tidak_sehat');
  });

  // ── Sangat Tidak Sehat ↔ Berbahaya (batas 300/301) ───────────────────────

  test('AQI = 300 → category "sangat_tidak_sehat" (batas atas)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(getAQICategory(300).category, 'sangat_tidak_sehat');
  });

  test('AQI = 301 → category "berbahaya" (batas bawah)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(getAQICategory(301).category, 'berbahaya');
  });

  // ── Batas atas global (500) ──────────────────────────────────────────────

  test('AQI = 500 → category "berbahaya" (batas atas skala)', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(getAQICategory(500).category, 'berbahaya');
  });
});
