/**
 * ISPU Calculator — PP No. 22 Tahun 2021
 * AQI = MAX dari semua parameter yang TERSEDIA (non-null).
 * Jika semua null → aqi = null, category = 'no_data'.
 */

const PM25_BREAKPOINTS = [
  { ispuLow: 0,   ispuHigh: 50,  concLow: 0,   concHigh: 55  },
  { ispuLow: 51,  ispuHigh: 100, concLow: 56,  concHigh: 150 },
  { ispuLow: 101, ispuHigh: 200, concLow: 151, concHigh: 250 },
  { ispuLow: 201, ispuHigh: 300, concLow: 251, concHigh: 350 },
  { ispuLow: 301, ispuHigh: 500, concLow: 351, concHigh: 500 },
];
const PM10_BREAKPOINTS = [
  { ispuLow: 0,   ispuHigh: 50,  concLow: 0,   concHigh: 50  },
  { ispuLow: 51,  ispuHigh: 100, concLow: 51,  concHigh: 150 },
  { ispuLow: 101, ispuHigh: 200, concLow: 151, concHigh: 350 },
  { ispuLow: 201, ispuHigh: 300, concLow: 351, concHigh: 420 },
  { ispuLow: 301, ispuHigh: 500, concLow: 421, concHigh: 500 },
];
const CO_BREAKPOINTS = [
  { ispuLow: 0,   ispuHigh: 50,  concLow: 0,     concHigh: 4000  },
  { ispuLow: 51,  ispuHigh: 100, concLow: 4001,  concHigh: 8000  },
  { ispuLow: 101, ispuHigh: 200, concLow: 8001,  concHigh: 15000 },
  { ispuLow: 201, ispuHigh: 300, concLow: 15001, concHigh: 30000 },
  { ispuLow: 301, ispuHigh: 500, concLow: 30001, concHigh: 45000 },
];
const SO2_BREAKPOINTS = [
  { ispuLow: 0,   ispuHigh: 50,  concLow: 0,    concHigh: 80   },
  { ispuLow: 51,  ispuHigh: 100, concLow: 81,   concHigh: 365  },
  { ispuLow: 101, ispuHigh: 200, concLow: 366,  concHigh: 800  },
  { ispuLow: 201, ispuHigh: 300, concLow: 801,  concHigh: 1600 },
  { ispuLow: 301, ispuHigh: 500, concLow: 1601, concHigh: 3000 },
];
const O3_BREAKPOINTS = [
  { ispuLow: 0,   ispuHigh: 50,  concLow: 0,   concHigh: 120  },
  { ispuLow: 51,  ispuHigh: 100, concLow: 121, concHigh: 235  },
  { ispuLow: 101, ispuHigh: 200, concLow: 236, concHigh: 400  },
  { ispuLow: 201, ispuHigh: 300, concLow: 401, concHigh: 800  },
  { ispuLow: 301, ispuHigh: 500, concLow: 801, concHigh: 1000 },
];
const NO2_BREAKPOINTS = [
  { ispuLow: 0,   ispuHigh: 50,  concLow: 0,    concHigh: 80   },
  { ispuLow: 51,  ispuHigh: 100, concLow: 81,   concHigh: 200  },
  { ispuLow: 101, ispuHigh: 200, concLow: 201,  concHigh: 1130 },
  { ispuLow: 201, ispuHigh: 300, concLow: 1131, concHigh: 2260 },
  { ispuLow: 301, ispuHigh: 500, concLow: 2261, concHigh: 3000 },
];

const isValidNumber = (v) =>
  typeof v === 'number' && Number.isFinite(v) && !Number.isNaN(v);

function calculateISPUFromConcentration(concentration, breakpoints) {
  if (!isValidNumber(concentration) || concentration < 0) return null;

  for (const bp of breakpoints) {
    if (concentration <= bp.concHigh) {
      const concRange = bp.concHigh - bp.concLow;
      if (concRange === 0) return bp.ispuHigh;
      const ispu =
        ((bp.ispuHigh - bp.ispuLow) / concRange) *
          (concentration - bp.concLow) +
        bp.ispuLow;
      return Math.round(Math.max(0, Math.min(500, ispu)));
    }
  }
  return 500;
}

const calculateISPUFromPM25 = (v) => calculateISPUFromConcentration(v, PM25_BREAKPOINTS);
const calculateISPUFromPM10 = (v) => calculateISPUFromConcentration(v, PM10_BREAKPOINTS);
const calculateISPUFromCO   = (v) => calculateISPUFromConcentration(v, CO_BREAKPOINTS);
const calculateISPUFromSO2  = (v) => calculateISPUFromConcentration(v, SO2_BREAKPOINTS);
const calculateISPUFromO3   = (v) => calculateISPUFromConcentration(v, O3_BREAKPOINTS);
const calculateISPUFromNO2  = (v) => calculateISPUFromConcentration(v, NO2_BREAKPOINTS);

/**
 * AQI = MAX dari parameter yang tersedia.
 * Semua parameter nullable. Bila SEMUA null → aqi: null, category: 'no_data'.
 * Tie-breaker: CO → SO2 → O3 → NO2 → PM2.5 → PM10
 */
function calculateAQI(pm25 = null, pm10 = null, co = null, so2 = null, o3 = null, no2 = null) {
  const hasPM25 = isValidNumber(pm25);
  const hasPM10 = isValidNumber(pm10);
  const hasCO   = isValidNumber(co);
  const hasSO2  = isValidNumber(so2);
  const hasO3   = isValidNumber(o3);
  const hasNO2  = isValidNumber(no2);

  if (!hasPM25 && !hasPM10 && !hasCO && !hasSO2 && !hasO3 && !hasNO2) {
    const cat = getAQICategory(null);
    return {
      aqi: null,
      ispuPM25: null, ispuPM10: null, ispuCO: null,
      ispuSO2: null, ispuO3: null, ispuNO2: null,
      dominant: null,
      category: cat.category,
      color: cat.color,
      label: cat.label,
    };
  }

  const ispuPM25 = hasPM25 ? calculateISPUFromPM25(pm25) : null;
  const ispuPM10 = hasPM10 ? calculateISPUFromPM10(pm10) : null;
  const ispuCO   = hasCO   ? calculateISPUFromCO(co)     : null;
  const ispuSO2  = hasSO2  ? calculateISPUFromSO2(so2)   : null;
  const ispuO3   = hasO3   ? calculateISPUFromO3(o3)     : null;
  const ispuNO2  = hasNO2  ? calculateISPUFromNO2(no2)   : null;

  const ordered = [
    ...(hasCO   ? [{ name: 'co',   value: ispuCO   }] : []),
    ...(hasSO2  ? [{ name: 'so2',  value: ispuSO2  }] : []),
    ...(hasO3   ? [{ name: 'o3',   value: ispuO3   }] : []),
    ...(hasNO2  ? [{ name: 'no2',  value: ispuNO2  }] : []),
    ...(hasPM25 ? [{ name: 'pm25', value: ispuPM25 }] : []),
    ...(hasPM10 ? [{ name: 'pm10', value: ispuPM10 }] : []),
  ];

  const aqi = Math.max(...ordered.map((c) => c.value));
  const dominant = ordered.find((c) => c.value === aqi)?.name || null;

  const cat = getAQICategory(aqi);

  return {
    aqi,
    ispuPM25, ispuPM10, ispuCO, ispuSO2, ispuO3, ispuNO2,
    dominant,
    category: cat.category,
    color: cat.color,
    label: cat.label,
  };
}

function getAQICategory(aqi) {
  if (aqi == null || Number.isNaN(aqi)) {
    return { category: 'no_data', color: '#BDBDBD', label: 'Tidak Ada Data' };
  }
  if (aqi <= 50)  return { category: 'baik',               color: '#00E400', label: 'Baik' };
  if (aqi <= 100) return { category: 'sedang',             color: '#0000FF', label: 'Sedang' };
  if (aqi <= 200) return { category: 'tidak_sehat',        color: '#FFFF00', label: 'Tidak Sehat' };
  if (aqi <= 300) return { category: 'sangat_tidak_sehat', color: '#FF0000', label: 'Sangat Tidak Sehat' };
  return { category: 'berbahaya', color: '#000000', label: 'Berbahaya' };
}

function aqiToConcentration(aqi, breakpoints) {
  if (!isValidNumber(aqi) || aqi <= 0) return 0;
  const target = Math.min(500, aqi);
  for (const bp of breakpoints) {
    if (target <= bp.ispuHigh) {
      const ispuRange = bp.ispuHigh - bp.ispuLow;
      if (ispuRange === 0) return bp.concLow;
      const ratio = (target - bp.ispuLow) / ispuRange;
      return Math.round(bp.concLow + ratio * (bp.concHigh - bp.concLow));
    }
  }
  return 500;
}

const aqiToPm25 = (v) => aqiToConcentration(v, PM25_BREAKPOINTS);
const aqiToSO2  = (v) => aqiToConcentration(v, SO2_BREAKPOINTS);
const aqiToO3   = (v) => aqiToConcentration(v, O3_BREAKPOINTS);
const aqiToNO2  = (v) => aqiToConcentration(v, NO2_BREAKPOINTS);

const evaluateParameter = (type, value) => {
  const catFor = (ispu) => getAQICategory(ispu);
  switch (type) {
    case 'pm25': { const i = calculateISPUFromPM25(value); const c = catFor(i); return { status: c.category, color: c.color, ispu: i }; }
    case 'pm10': { const i = calculateISPUFromPM10(value); const c = catFor(i); return { status: c.category, color: c.color, ispu: i }; }
    case 'co':   { const i = calculateISPUFromCO(value);   const c = catFor(i); return { status: c.category, color: c.color, ispu: i }; }
    case 'so2':  { const i = calculateISPUFromSO2(value);  const c = catFor(i); return { status: c.category, color: c.color, ispu: i }; }
    case 'o3':   { const i = calculateISPUFromO3(value);   const c = catFor(i); return { status: c.category, color: c.color, ispu: i }; }
    case 'no2':  { const i = calculateISPUFromNO2(value);  const c = catFor(i); return { status: c.category, color: c.color, ispu: i }; }
    case 'temperature':
      if (value == null) return { status: 'unknown', color: '#CCCCCC' };
      if (value >= 24 && value <= 26) return { status: 'ideal', color: '#0066CC' };
      if (value >= 20 && value <= 28) return { status: 'normal', color: '#00E400' };
      if (value >= 18 && value <= 30) return { status: 'sedang', color: '#FFFF00' };
      return { status: 'tidak_ideal', color: '#FF7E00' };
    case 'humidity':
      if (value == null) return { status: 'unknown', color: '#CCCCCC' };
      if (value >= 40 && value <= 60) return { status: 'ideal', color: '#0066CC' };
      if (value >= 30 && value <= 70) return { status: 'normal', color: '#00E400' };
      if (value >= 20 && value <= 80) return { status: 'sedang', color: '#FFFF00' };
      return { status: 'tidak_ideal', color: '#FF7E00' };
    default:
      return { status: 'unknown', color: '#CCCCCC' };
  }
};

module.exports = {
  calculateISPUFromPM25,
  calculateISPUFromPM10,
  calculateISPUFromCO,
  calculateISPUFromSO2,
  calculateISPUFromO3,
  calculateISPUFromNO2,
  calculateAQI,
  getAQICategory,
  evaluateParameter,
  aqiToPm25,
  aqiToSO2,
  aqiToO3,
  aqiToNO2,
  calculateAQIFromPM25: (pm25) => {
    const ispu = calculateISPUFromPM25(pm25);
    if (ispu == null) {
      const cat = getAQICategory(null);
      return { aqi: null, category: cat.category, color: cat.color };
    }
    const cat = getAQICategory(ispu);
    return { aqi: ispu, category: cat.category, color: cat.color };
  },
};

C:\Users\fatha\Develop\UNILA-AQI\unila-aqi-backend\src\utils\auth.js

const jwt = require('jsonwebtoken');
const bcrypt = require('bcryptjs');

// Generate JWT token
const generateToken = (userId, username, role) => {
  return jwt.sign(
    { id: userId, username, role },
    process.env.JWT_SECRET,
    { expiresIn: '7d' }
  );
};

// Hash password
const hashPassword = async (password) => {
  const salt = await bcrypt.genSalt(10);
  return await bcrypt.hash(password, salt);
};

// Compare password
const comparePassword = async (password, hashedPassword) => {
  return await bcrypt.compare(password, hashedPassword);
};

// Verify JWT token
const verifyToken = (token) => {
  try {
    return jwt.verify(token, process.env.JWT_SECRET);
  } catch (error) {
    return null;
  }
};

module.exports = {
  generateToken,
  hashPassword,
  comparePassword,
  verifyToken
};

const nodemailer = require('nodemailer');

// ============================================================
// KONFIGURASI EMAIL (SENDCOREX)
// ============================================================
const SMTP_HOST = process.env.SMTP_HOST || 'smtp.emailsbit.com';
const SMTP_PORT = parseInt(process.env.SMTP_PORT || '588', 10);
const SMTP_USER = process.env.SMTP_USERNAME;
const SMTP_PASS = process.env.SMTP_PASSWORD; 
const SMTP_FROM = process.env.SMTP_FROM || 'unilaaqi@inboxtaken.com';

if (!SMTP_USER || !SMTP_PASS) {
  console.error('❌ FATAL: SMTP_USERNAME dan SMTP_PASSWORD wajib di-set di Render Environment Variables.');
  process.exit(1);
}

// ============================================================
// SETUP TRANSPORTER
// ============================================================
const transporter = nodemailer.createTransport({
  host: SMTP_HOST,
  port: SMTP_PORT,
  secure: false,       // Wajib false untuk STARTTLS (port 588)
  requireTLS: true,    // Memaksa koneksi dienkripsi via STARTTLS
  auth: {
    user: SMTP_USER,
    pass: SMTP_PASS,
  },
  connectionTimeout: 15000, // Toleransi 15 detik agar tidak langsung timeout
  greetingTimeout: 10000,
  socketTimeout: 20000,
  tls: {
    rejectUnauthorized: false,
    minVersion: 'TLSv1.2'
  },
  // AKTIFKAN LOG UNTUK DEBUGGING DI RENDER LOGS
  logger: true, 
  debug: true   
});

// Verifikasi koneksi saat server menyala
transporter.verify((error, success) => {
  if (error) {
    console.error('⚠️  SMTP transporter verification failed!');
    console.error('Detail Error:', error.message);
    if (error.code) console.error('Error Code:', error.code);
  } else {
    console.log('✅ SMTP transporter ready (EmailSbit/Sendcorex)');
  }
});

// ============================================================
// HELPER FUNCTIONS
// ============================================================

const Building = require('../models/Building');
const Room = require('../models/Room');
const IoTDevice = require('../models/IoTDevice');

const sampleBuildings = [
  {
    name: 'Gedung H',
    code: 'H',
    description: 'Gedung Fakultas Teknik'
  },
  {
    name: 'Gedung M',
    code: 'M',
    description: 'Gedung Fakultas MIPA'
  },
  {
    name: 'Gedung A',
    code: 'A',
    description: 'Gedung Administrasi'
  },
  {
    name: 'Perpustakaan Pusat',
    code: 'LIB',
    description: 'Perpustakaan Universitas'
  }
];

const sampleRooms = [
  // Gedung H
  { name: 'H101', buildingCode: 'H' },
  { name: 'H102', buildingCode: 'H' },
  { name: 'H201', buildingCode: 'H' },
  { name: 'Lab Komputer H', buildingCode: 'H' },
  
  // Gedung M
  { name: 'M101', buildingCode: 'M' },
  { name: 'M102', buildingCode: 'M' },
  { name: 'Lab Kimia M', buildingCode: 'M' },
  
  // Gedung A
  { name: 'A101', buildingCode: 'A' },
  { name: 'Ruang Rapat A', buildingCode: 'A' },
  
  // Perpustakaan
  { name: 'Lobi Perpustakaan', buildingCode: 'LIB' },
  { name: 'Ruang Baca', buildingCode: 'LIB' }
];

const seedSampleData = async () => {
  try {
    console.log('🌱 Seeding sample data...');

    // CREATE BUILDINGS tanpa menggunakan save() yang trigger hooks
    const buildings = {};
    
    for (const buildingData of sampleBuildings) {
      try {
        // Cek apakah building sudah ada
        const existingBuilding = await Building.findOne({ code: buildingData.code });
        
        if (existingBuilding) {
          console.log(`⏩ Building ${buildingData.code} already exists, skipping`);
          buildings[buildingData.code] = existingBuilding;
          continue;
        }

        // Gunakan insertOne untuk bypass hooks
        const result = await Building.collection.insertOne({
          ...buildingData,
          roomCount: 0,
          createdAt: new Date(),
          updatedAt: new Date()
        });
        
        const building = await Building.findById(result.insertedId);
        buildings[buildingData.code] = building;
        console.log(`✅ Created building: ${building.name} (${building.code})`);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error(`❌ Error creating building ${buildingData.code}:`, error.message);
      }
    }

    // CREATE ROOMS dengan buildingName yang benar.
    // Ruangan hasil seed belum punya device IoT, jadi semua nilai data
    // dikosongkan (null) sampai admin memilih device pada ruangan tsb.
    let roomCount = 0;
    for (const roomData of sampleRooms) {
      try {
        const building = buildings[roomData.buildingCode];
        if (building) {
          // Cek apakah room sudah ada
          const existingRoom = await Room.findOne({ 
            name: roomData.name, 
            building: building._id 
          });
          
          if (existingRoom) {
            console.log(`⏩ Room ${roomData.name} already exists, skipping`);
            continue;
          }

          // Gunakan insertOne untuk bypass hooks dengan buildingName yang benar
          const result = await Room.collection.insertOne({
            name: roomData.name,
            building: building._id,
            buildingName: building.name,
            iotDeviceId: null,
            currentAQI: null,
            currentData: {
              pm25: null,
              pm10: null,
              co: null,
              so2: null,
              o3: null,
              no2: null,
              temperature: null,
              humidity: null,
              updatedAt: new Date()
            },
            demoMode: false,
            createdAt: new Date(),
            updatedAt: new Date()
          });
          
          // Update building room count secara manual
          await Building.collection.updateOne(
            { _id: building._id },
            { $inc: { roomCount: 1 } }
          );
          
          roomCount++;
          console.log(`✅ Created room: ${roomData.name} in ${building.name}`);
        }
      } catch (error) {
        console.error(`❌ Error creating room ${roomData.name}:`, error.message);
      }
    }

    console.log(`🎉 Sample data seeding completed! Created ${roomCount} rooms.`);
    return { success: true, message: `Created ${roomCount} rooms` };
    
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('❌ Error seeding sample data:', error);
    return { success: false, message: error.message };
  }
};

const clearSampleData = async () => {
  try {
    await Room.deleteMany({});
    await Building.deleteMany({});
    console.log('🗑️  Sample data cleared');
    return { success: true, message: 'Data cleared' };
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('❌ Error clearing data:', error);
    return { success: false, message: error.message };
  }
};

// Fungsi untuk sync building names
const syncBuildingNames = async () => {
  try {
    console.log('🔄 Syncing building names in rooms...');
    
    const rooms = await Room.find().populate('building', 'name');
    let updatedCount = 0;
    
    for (const room of rooms) {
      if (room.building && room.buildingName !== room.building.name) {
        room.buildingName = room.building.name;
        await room.save();
        updatedCount++;
      }
    }
    
    console.log(`✅ Synced building names for ${updatedCount} rooms`);
    return { success: true, message: `Synced ${updatedCount} rooms` };
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('❌ Error syncing building names:', error);
    return { success: false, message: error.message };
  }
};

// PERHATIAN: Ekspor dengan nama yang benar!
module.exports = {
  seedSampleData,
  clearSampleData,
  syncBuildingNames  // Tambahkan fungsi sync
};

C:\Users\fatha\Develop\UNILA-AQI\unila-aqi-backend\src\utils\serviceLock.js

/**
 * Service lock — memastikan hanya SATU siklus service
 * (iot) yang berjalan pada satu waktu.
 *
 * Tujuan: output terminal tidak saling memotong antar siklus.
 *
 * Cara kerja:
 *   - Setiap pemanggil `runExclusive(fn)` mengantre pada promise chain.
 *   - `fn` dijalankan setelah semua pemanggil sebelumnya selesai.
 *   - Jika `fn` melempar error, promise tetap resolve (tidak merusak chain).
 */

let chain = Promise.resolve();

function runExclusive(fn) {
  const run = chain.then(fn, fn); // jalankan fn baik chain resolve maupun reject
  // chain berikutnya menunggu `run` selesai (success/fail), tanpa
  // meneruskan rejection agar 1 error tidak "meracuni" pemanggil berikutnya.
  chain = run.then(
    () => undefined,
    () => undefined
  );
  return run;
}

module.exports = { runExclusive };

// ============================================================

// ============================================================
const app = express();
const server = http.createServer(app);

// Handler error di level HTTP server (mis. port bentrok)
server.on('error', (err) => {
  console.error('❌ Server error:', err.message);
  if (err.code === 'EADDRINUSE') {
    console.error(`   Port ${PORT} sudah digunakan.`);
    console.error('   Hentikan proses lain di port tersebut atau ubah PORT di .env');
  }
  process.exit(1);
});

const io = socketIo(server, {
  cors: {
    origin: '*',
    methods: ['GET', 'POST', 'PUT', 'DELETE'],
    credentials: true,
  },
  // ✅ Timeout lebih longgar untuk koneksi mobile (jaringan tidak stabil)
  pingTimeout: 60000,
  pingInterval: 25000,
});

// ============================================================
// 3) MIDDLEWARE
// ============================================================
app.use(cors());
app.use(express.json({ limit: '1mb' })); // batasi payload agar tidak disalahgunakan
app.set('socketio', io);

// ============================================================
// 4) ROUTES
// ============================================================
app.use('/api/auth', authRoutes);
app.use('/api/seed', seedRoutes);
app.use('/api/test', testRoutes);
app.use('/api/buildings', buildingRoutes);
app.use('/api/rooms', roomRoutes);
app.use('/api/iot-devices', iotDeviceRoutes);
app.use('/api/sensor-data', sensorDataRoutes);

// Basic route (health check)
app.get('/', (req, res) => {
  res.json({
    message: 'UNILA AQI Backend API is running',
    database: mongoose.connection.readyState === 1 ? 'Connected' : 'Disconnected',
    timestamp: new Date(),
    socketIO: 'Active',
    env: process.env.NODE_ENV || 'development',
  });
});

app.get('/api/simple-test', (req, res) => {
  res.json({
    message: 'Simple test endpoint working',
    timestamp: new Date(),
    status: 'OK',
    dbConnected: mongoose.connection.readyState === 1,
    socketConnected: io !== undefined,
  });
});

// ============================================================
// 5) SOCKET.IO CONNECTION HANDLER
// ============================================================
io.on('connection', (socket) => {
  console.log(`🔌 New client connected: ${socket.id}`);

  socket.on('join-room', (roomId) => {
    socket.join(roomId);
    console.log(`📡 Socket ${socket.id} joined room: ${roomId}`);
    socket.emit('room-joined', {
      roomId: roomId,
      message: `Connected to room ${roomId}`,
      timestamp: new Date(),
    });
  });

  socket.on('leave-room', (roomId) => {
    socket.leave(roomId);
    console.log(`📡 Socket ${socket.id} left room: ${roomId}`);
  });

  socket.on('subscribe-dashboard', () => {
    socket.join('dashboard');
    console.log(`📊 Socket ${socket.id} subscribed to dashboard updates`);
    socket.emit('dashboard-subscribed', {
      message: 'Subscribed to dashboard updates',
      timestamp: new Date(),
    });
  });

  socket.on('unsubscribe-dashboard', () => {
    socket.leave('dashboard');
    console.log(`📊 Socket ${socket.id} unsubscribed from dashboard`);
  });

  socket.on('subscribe-room-updates', (roomId) => {
    socket.join(`room-updates-${roomId}`);
    console.log(`📡 Socket ${socket.id} subscribed to room ${roomId} updates`);
  });

  socket.on('request-refresh', (data) => {
    const roomId = data?.roomId;
    console.log(`🔄 Socket ${socket.id} requested refresh${roomId ? ` for room ${roomId}` : ''}`);

    if (roomId) {
      socket.to(roomId).emit('room-refresh-requested', {
        requestedBy: socket.id,
        timestamp: new Date(),
      });
    } else {
      iotService.pollAllIoTDevices().catch((e) => {
        console.error('❌ iotService.pollAllIoTDevices error:', e.message);
      });

      io.emit('refresh-started', {
        requestedBy: socket.id,
        timestamp: new Date(),
      });
    }

    socket.emit('refresh-acknowledged', {
      message: 'Refresh request received',
      timestamp: new Date(),
    });
  });

  socket.on('disconnect', (reason) => {
    console.log(`🔌 Client disconnected: ${socket.id} (reason: ${reason})`);
    socket.to('dashboard').emit('client-disconnected', {
      socketId: socket.id,
      timestamp: new Date(),
      reason: reason,
    });
  });

  socket.on('ping', (data) => {
    const clientTime = data?.timestamp;
    socket.emit('pong', {
      serverTime: new Date(),
      clientTime: clientTime,
      latency: clientTime ? Date.now() - new Date(clientTime).getTime() : null,
    });
  });

  socket.emit('connected', {
    socketId: socket.id,
    message: 'Connected to UNILA AQI WebSocket server',
    timestamp: new Date(),
    serverInfo: {
      name: 'UNILA AQI Backend',
      version: '1.0.0',
      features: ['real-time-updates', 'room-monitoring', 'dashboard-updates'],
    },
  });
});

// ============================================================
// 6) HELPER BROADCAST (opsional — dipakai modul lain bila perlu)
// ============================================================
const broadcastRoomUpdate = (roomId, data) => {
  if (io) {
    io.to(roomId).emit('room-update', {
      roomId,
      data,
      timestamp: new Date(),
    });
    console.log(`📢 Broadcast update to room ${roomId}: AQI ${data.aqi}`);
  }
};

const broadcastToAll = (event, data) => {
  if (io) {
    io.emit(event, data);
  }
};

module.exports = { broadcastRoomUpdate, broadcastToAll };

// ============================================================
// 7) SEED ADMIN & SYNC HELPERS
// ============================================================
async function syncBuildingNames() {
  try {
    console.log('🔄 Syncing building names in rooms...');
    const rooms = await Room.find().populate('building', 'name');
    let updatedCount = 0;

    for (const room of rooms) {
      if (room.building && room.buildingName !== room.building.name) {
        room.buildingName = room.building.name;
        await room.save();
        updatedCount++;
      }
    }

    if (updatedCount > 0) {
      console.log(`✅ Synced building names for ${updatedCount} rooms`);
    } else {
      console.log('✅ All room building names are already synced');
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('❌ Error syncing building names:', error.message);
  }
}

const seedInitialAdmin = async () => {
  try {
    const { hashPassword } = require('./utils/auth');
    const adminExists = await User.findOne({ role: 'admin' });

    if (!adminExists) {
      const hashedPassword = await hashPassword('admin123');
      const admin = new User({
        username: 'admin',
        password: hashedPassword,
        email: 'admin@unila-aqi.ac.id',
        role: 'admin',
      });

      await admin.save();
      console.log('✅ Initial admin user created');
      console.log('👤 Username: admin');
      console.log('🔑 Password: admin123');
    } else {
      console.log('✅ Admin user already exists');
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('❌ Error seeding admin:', error);
  }
};

      success: true,

      timestamp: new Date(),
      services: {
        iot: 'Forced polling completed',
      },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('❌ Force refresh error:', error);
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Gagal melakukan refresh',
      error: error.message,
    });
  }
});

  res.status(404).json({
    success: false,
    message: 'Endpoint not found',
    path: req.originalUrl,
    method: req.method,
  });
});

app.use((err, req, res, next) => {
  console.error('❌ Unhandled error:', err.message);
  res.status(err.status || 500).json({
    success: false,
    message: err.message || 'Internal server error',
  });
});

// ============================================================
// 10) MONGODB CONNECT + START SERVER
// ============================================================
// ✅ KUNCI PERUBAHAN:
//   - Semua inisialisasi (syncIndexes, seed, start service)
//     dijalankan SETELAH MongoDB tersambung.
//   - server.listen dipanggil SETELAH semuanya siap.
//   - Kalau koneksi DB gagal, proses keluar (exit 1) sehingga
//     Render tahu deploy gagal dan tidak meneruskan trafik.

mongoose
  .connect(process.env.MONGODB_URI, {
    serverSelectionTimeoutMS: 15000, // toleransi cold start Atlas
  })
  .then(async () => {
    console.log('✅ Connected to MongoDB Atlas');
    console.log(`📊 Database: ${mongoose.connection.db.databaseName}`);
    console.log(`📍 Host: ${mongoose.connection.host}`);

    // Drop index lama & buat ulang yang sparse untuk Building.code
    try {
      await Building.syncIndexes();
      console.log('✅ Building indexes synced (code sparse)');
    } catch (syncErr) {
      console.error('⚠️  Building index sync warning:', syncErr.message);
    }

    // Seed admin & sync building names
    await seedInitialAdmin();
    await syncBuildingNames();

    // Seed sample buildings & rooms jika kosong
    const buildingCount = await Building.countDocuments();
    if (buildingCount === 0) {
      console.log('🌱 Seeding sample data...');
      await seedSampleData();
    } else {
      console.log(`📊 Database already has ${buildingCount} buildings, skipping sample seeding`);
    }

    // Start background services
    iotService.start(io);

    console.log('✅ All services started');
    console.log('   - IoT Service: ACTIVE (30 second intervals)');

    // ✅ START SERVER SETELAH SEMUA SIAP
    server.listen(PORT, HOST, () => {
      console.log('═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════');
      console.log(`🚀 Server running on http://${HOST}:${PORT}`);
      console.log(`🌐 API Base URL: /api`);
      console.log(`📡 WebSocket ready`);
      console.log(`🔌 Socket.IO ready for real-time updates`);
      console.log(`🌍 Environment: ${process.env.NODE_ENV || 'development'}`);
      console.log('═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════');
    });
  })
  .catch((err) => {
    console.error('❌ MongoDB connection error:', err.message);
    const uri = process.env.MONGODB_URI || '';
    const maskedUri = uri.replace(/:[^:@]*@/, ':****@');
    console.error('Connection string:', maskedUri);
    console.error('⚠️  Server tidak akan dijalankan sampai DB tersambung.');
    process.exit(1); // ✅ Render akan tahu deploy gagal
  });

// ============================================================
// 11) GRACEFUL SHUTDOWN (penting untuk Render)
// ============================================================
// Render mengirim SIGTERM saat:
//   - deploy versi baru (container lama dimatikan)
//   - manual restart
//   - scale down
// Kita beri waktu 10 detik untuk menutup koneksi DB & socket,
// lalu exit. Tanpa ini, Render akan force-kill setelah 30 detik
// dan mungkin menyisakan koneksi "zombie" di MongoDB Atlas.

let isShuttingDown = false;

const shutdown = async (signal) => {
  if (isShuttingDown) return;
  isShuttingDown = true;

  console.log(`\n⚠️  Received ${signal}. Shutting down gracefully...`);

  // 1) Stop background services
  try {
    iotService.stop();
    console.log('⏹️  Background services stopped');
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('⚠️  Error stopping services:', e.message);
  }

  // 2) Tutup Socket.IO
  try {
    io.close(() => console.log('🔌 Socket.IO closed'));
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('⚠️  Error closing Socket.IO:', e.message);
  }

  // 3) Tutup HTTP server
  server.close(() => {
    console.log('🛑 HTTP server closed');
  });

  // 4) Disconnect MongoDB
  try {
    await mongoose.disconnect();
    console.log('🔌 MongoDB disconnected');
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('⚠️  Error disconnecting MongoDB:', e.message);
  }

  // 5) Paksa exit setelah 10 detik jika masih menggantung
  setTimeout(() => {
    console.error('⏱️  Forcing exit after 10s');
    process.exit(1);
  }, 10000).unref();

  process.exit(0);
};

process.on('SIGTERM', () => shutdown('SIGTERM'));
process.on('SIGINT', () => shutdown('SIGINT'));

// Tangkap unhandled rejection agar tidak crash diam-diam
process.on('unhandledRejection', (reason) => {
  console.error('❌ Unhandled Rejection:', reason);
});

process.on('uncaughtException', (err) => {
  console.error('❌ Uncaught Exception:', err);
  // Jangan langsung exit — biarkan graceful shutdown jika ada
  shutdown('uncaughtException');
});

const mongoose = require('mongoose');
const { seedSampleData, clearSampleData } = require('./utils/seedData');
const User = require('./models/User');
const { hashPassword } = require('./utils/auth');
require('dotenv').config();

async function resetDatabase() {
  try {
    console.log('🔄 Resetting database...');
    
    // Connect to MongoDB Atlas - SIMPLIFIED
    await mongoose.connect(process.env.MONGODB_URI);
    
    console.log('✅ Connected to MongoDB Atlas');
    console.log(`📊 Database: ${mongoose.connection.db.databaseName}`);
    
    // Clear all existing data
    console.log('🗑️  Clearing existing data...');
    await clearSampleData();
    
    // Seed admin user
    console.log('👤 Creating admin user...');
    const adminExists = await User.findOne({ role: 'admin' });
    
    if (!adminExists) {
      const hashedPassword = await hashPassword('admin123');
      const admin = new User({
        username: 'admin',
        password: hashedPassword,
        email: 'admin@unila-aqi.ac.id',
        role: 'admin'
      });
      
      await admin.save();
      console.log('✅ Admin user created');
      console.log('👤 Username: admin');
      console.log('🔑 Password: admin123');
    } else {
      console.log('✅ Admin user already exists');
    }
    
    // Seed sample data
    console.log('🌱 Seeding sample data...');
    const result = await seedSampleData();
    
    console.log('🎉 Database reset completed!');
    console.log(result.message || '');
    
    // Count documents
    const Building = require('./models/Building');
    const Room = require('./models/Room');
    const IoTDevice = require('./models/IoTDevice');
    
    const buildingCount = await Building.countDocuments();
    const roomCount = await Room.countDocuments();
    const deviceCount = await IoTDevice.countDocuments();
    
    console.log('\n📊 Database Statistics:');
    console.log(`🏢 Buildings: ${buildingCount}`);
    console.log(`🚪 Rooms: ${roomCount}`);
    console.log(`📡 IoT Devices: ${deviceCount}`);
    
    // Show sample data
    console.log('\n🏢 Sample Buildings:');
    const buildings = await Building.find().limit(3);
    buildings.forEach(b => {
      console.log(`   - ${b.name} (${b.code || 'No code'})`);
    });
    
    console.log('\n🚪 Sample Rooms:');
    const rooms = await Room.find().populate('building', 'name').limit(3);
    rooms.forEach(r => {
      console.log(`   - ${r.name} in ${r.building?.name || r.buildingName}`);
    });
    
    process.exit(0);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('❌ Error resetting database:', error.message);
    console.error('Stack:', error.stack);
    
    // Show connection string for debugging
    const uri = process.env.MONGODB_URI || '';
    const maskedUri = uri.replace(/:[^:@]*@/, ':****@');
    console.error('Connection string:', maskedUri);
    
    process.exit(1);
  }
}

resetDatabase();

const http = require('http');
const url = require('url');

// ============================================================
// ISPU Calculator (sama persis dengan backend aqiCalculator.js)
// ============================================================
const PM25_BREAKPOINTS = [
  { ispuLow: 0,   ispuHigh: 50,  concLow: 0,   concHigh: 55  },
  { ispuLow: 51,  ispuHigh: 100, concLow: 56,  concHigh: 150 },
  { ispuLow: 101, ispuHigh: 200, concLow: 151, concHigh: 250 },
  { ispuLow: 201, ispuHigh: 300, concLow: 251, concHigh: 350 },
  { ispuLow: 301, ispuHigh: 500, concLow: 351, concHigh: 500 },
];

const PM10_BREAKPOINTS = [
  { ispuLow: 0,   ispuHigh: 50,  concLow: 0,   concHigh: 50  },
  { ispuLow: 51,  ispuHigh: 100, concLow: 51,  concHigh: 150 },
  { ispuLow: 101, ispuHigh: 200, concLow: 151, concHigh: 350 },
  { ispuLow: 201, ispuHigh: 300, concLow: 351, concHigh: 420 },
  { ispuLow: 301, ispuHigh: 500, concLow: 421, concHigh: 500 },
];

const CO_BREAKPOINTS = [
  { ispuLow: 0,   ispuHigh: 50,  concLow: 0,     concHigh: 4000  },
  { ispuLow: 51,  ispuHigh: 100, concLow: 4001,  concHigh: 8000  },
  { ispuLow: 101, ispuHigh: 200, concLow: 8001,  concHigh: 15000 },
  { ispuLow: 201, ispuHigh: 300, concLow: 15001, concHigh: 30000 },
  { ispuLow: 301, ispuHigh: 500, concLow: 30001, concHigh: 45000 },
];

const SO2_BREAKPOINTS = [
  { ispuLow: 0,   ispuHigh: 50,  concLow: 0,    concHigh: 80   },
  { ispuLow: 51,  ispuHigh: 100, concLow: 81,   concHigh: 365  },
  { ispuLow: 101, ispuHigh: 200, concLow: 366,  concHigh: 800  },
  { ispuLow: 201, ispuHigh: 300, concLow: 801,  concHigh: 1600 },
  { ispuLow: 301, ispuHigh: 500, concLow: 1601, concHigh: 3000 },
];

const O3_BREAKPOINTS = [
  { ispuLow: 0,   ispuHigh: 50,  concLow: 0,   concHigh: 120  },
  { ispuLow: 51,  ispuHigh: 100, concLow: 121, concHigh: 235  },
  { ispuLow: 101, ispuHigh: 200, concLow: 236, concHigh: 400  },
  { ispuLow: 201, ispuHigh: 300, concLow: 401, concHigh: 800  },
  { ispuLow: 301, ispuHigh: 500, concLow: 801, concHigh: 1000 },
];

const NO2_BREAKPOINTS = [
  { ispuLow: 0,   ispuHigh: 50,  concLow: 0,    concHigh: 80   },
  { ispuLow: 51,  ispuHigh: 100, concLow: 81,   concHigh: 200  },
  { ispuLow: 101, ispuHigh: 200, concLow: 201,  concHigh: 1130 },
  { ispuLow: 201, ispuHigh: 300, concLow: 1131, concHigh: 2260 },
  { ispuLow: 301, ispuHigh: 500, concLow: 2261, concHigh: 3000 },
];

function calculateISPU(conc, bps) {
  if (conc == null || isNaN(conc) || conc < 0) return 0;
  for (const bp of bps) {
    if (conc <= bp.concHigh) {
      const range = bp.concHigh - bp.concLow;
      if (range === 0) return bp.ispuHigh;
      const ispu = ((bp.ispuHigh - bp.ispuLow) / range) * (conc - bp.concLow) + bp.ispuLow;
      return Math.round(Math.max(0, Math.min(500, ispu)));
    }
  }
  return 500;
}

const calculateISPUFromPM25 = (pm25) => calculateISPU(pm25, PM25_BREAKPOINTS);
const calculateISPUFromPM10 = (pm10) => calculateISPU(pm10, PM10_BREAKPOINTS);
const calculateISPUFromCO   = (co)   => calculateISPU(co,   CO_BREAKPOINTS);
const calculateISPUFromSO2  = (so2)  => calculateISPU(so2,  SO2_BREAKPOINTS);
const calculateISPUFromO3   = (o3)   => calculateISPU(o3,   O3_BREAKPOINTS);
const calculateISPUFromNO2  = (no2)  => calculateISPU(no2,  NO2_BREAKPOINTS);

    co:          { min: 600, max: 2500, mean: 1200 },
    so2:         { min: 20, max: 80, mean: 40 },
    o3:          { min: 30, max: 100, mean: 60 },
    no2:         { min: 20, max: 80, mean: 50 },
    temperature: { min: 24, max: 28, mean: 26  },
    humidity:    { min: 60, max: 80, mean: 70  },
  },
  '20-06': {
    pm25:        { min: 5,  max: 15, mean: 9   },
    pm10:        { min: 10, max: 30, mean: 18  },
    co:          { min: 300, max: 1500, mean: 800 },
    so2:         { min: 10, max: 50, mean: 25 },
    o3:          { min: 10, max: 60, mean: 30 },
    no2:         { min: 10, max: 50, mean: 30 },
    temperature: { min: 22, max: 26, mean: 24  },
    humidity:    { min: 70, max: 90, mean: 80  },
  },
};

function getTimeSlot(hour) {
  if (hour >= 6  && hour < 8)  return '06-08';
  if (hour >= 8  && hour < 12) return '08-12';
  if (hour >= 12 && hour < 16) return '12-16';
  if (hour >= 16 && hour < 18) return '16-18';
  if (hour >= 18 && hour < 20) return '18-20';
  return '20-06';
}

function randomInRange(min, max, mean) {
  const mode = Math.max(min, Math.min(max, mean ?? (min + max) / 2));
  const u = Math.random();
  const f = (mode - min) / (max - min);
  if (u < f) return min + Math.sqrt(u * (max - min) * (mode - min));
  return max - Math.sqrt((1 - u) * (max - min) * (max - mode));
}

// In-memory storage
const registeredDevices = new Map();
const deviceStates = new Map();
const secondDataCache = new Map();

// ============================================================
// Anomaly simulation (SAMA dengan backend dataGenerator)
// 92% normal / 5% spike (8-17x) / 3% error
// ============================================================
function applyAnomaly(pm25, pm10, co, so2, o3, no2, temperature, humidity) {
  const roll = Math.random();

  if (roll < 0.05) {
    const subRoll = Math.random();
    let multiplier;
    if (subRoll < 0.60) {
      multiplier = 8 + Math.random() * 2;   // 8-10x
    } else if (subRoll < 0.90) {
      multiplier = 10 + Math.random() * 3;  // 10-13x
    } else {
      multiplier = 13 + Math.random() * 4;  // 13-17x
    }

    return {
      isAnomaly: true,
      anomalyType: 'spike',
      pm25: pm25 * multiplier,
      pm10: pm10 * multiplier,
      co: co * (1.5 + Math.random()), // Spike CO 1.5x - 2.5x dari nilai normal
      so2: so2 * multiplier,
      o3: o3 * multiplier,
      no2: no2 * multiplier,
      temperature,
      humidity,
    };
  }

  if (roll < 0.08) {
    return {
      isAnomaly: true,
      anomalyType: 'error',
      pm25: -1, pm10: -1, co: -1,
      so2: -1, o3: -1, no2: -1,
      temperature: -1, humidity: -1,
    };
  }

  return {
    isAnomaly: false,
    anomalyType: null,
    pm25, pm10, co, so2, o3, no2, temperature, humidity,
  };
}

// Helper: Generate data per 30 detik
function generate30SecondBasedData(deviceId) {
  const now = new Date();
  const currentSecond = Math.floor(now.getTime() / 1000);
  const current30SecondBlock = Math.floor(currentSecond / 30);
  const hour = now.getHours();
  const minute = now.getMinutes();
  const second = now.getSeconds();

  // Inisialisasi state device
  if (!deviceStates.has(deviceId)) {
    const hash = deviceId.split('').reduce((acc, c) => acc + c.charCodeAt(0), 0);
    deviceStates.set(deviceId, {
      roomType: hash % 4,
      prevValues: null,
      last30SecondBlock: current30SecondBlock - 1,
      updateCounter: 0,
    });
    console.log(`📊 Init device ${deviceId}:`, {
      roomType: ['Kelas', 'Laboratorium', 'Perpustakaan', 'Aula'][deviceStates.get(deviceId).roomType],
    });
  }

  const state = deviceStates.get(deviceId);

  //Cache 30 detik
  if (secondDataCache.has(deviceId)) {
   const cached = secondDataCache.get(deviceId);
   if (cached.block30 === current30SecondBlock) {
     return cached.data;
   }
  }

  const slot = getTimeSlot(hour);
  const r = TIME_RANGES[slot];

  const rawPm25 = randomInRange(r.pm25.min, r.pm25.max, r.pm25.mean);
  const rawPm10 = randomInRange(r.pm10.min, r.pm10.max, r.pm10.mean);
  const rawCo   = randomInRange(r.co.min,  r.co.max,  r.co.mean);
  const rawSo2  = randomInRange(r.so2.min, r.so2.max, r.so2.mean);
  const rawO3   = randomInRange(r.o3.min,  r.o3.max,  r.o3.mean);
  const rawNo2  = randomInRange(r.no2.min, r.no2.max, r.no2.mean);
  const rawTemp = randomInRange(r.temperature.min, r.temperature.max, r.temperature.mean);
  const rawRh   = randomInRange(r.humidity.min,    r.humidity.max,    r.humidity.mean);

  const alpha = 0.4;
  const prev = state.prevValues || {
    pm25: rawPm25, pm10: rawPm10, co: rawCo,
    so2: rawSo2, o3: rawO3, no2: rawNo2,
    temperature: rawTemp, humidity: rawRh,
  };
  const pm25 = prev.pm25 * (1 - alpha) + rawPm25 * alpha;
  const pm10 = prev.pm10 * (1 - alpha) + rawPm10 * alpha;
  const co   = prev.co  * (1 - alpha) + rawCo  * alpha;
  const so2  = prev.so2 * (1 - alpha) + rawSo2 * alpha;
  const o3   = prev.o3  * (1 - alpha) + rawO3  * alpha;
  const no2  = prev.no2 * (1 - alpha) + rawNo2 * alpha;
  const temperature = prev.temperature * (1 - alpha) + rawTemp * alpha;
  const humidity    = prev.humidity    * (1 - alpha) + rawRh   * alpha;

  // Update state (JANGAN feed anomali ke state — supaya spike transien 1 tick)
  state.prevValues = { pm25, pm10, co, so2, o3, no2, temperature, humidity };
  state.updateCounter++;
  state.last30SecondBlock = current30SecondBlock;

  const anomalous = applyAnomaly(pm25, pm10, co, so2, o3, no2, temperature, humidity);

  // Hitung AQI — konsisten dengan backend aqiCalculator.js
  let finalAQI, finalCategory, dominant;
  if (anomalous.anomalyType === 'error') {
    finalAQI = -1;
    finalCategory = 'error';
    dominant = null;
  } else {
    const ispuPM25 = calculateISPUFromPM25(anomalous.pm25);
    const ispuPM10 = calculateISPUFromPM10(anomalous.pm10);
    const ispuCO   = calculateISPUFromCO(anomalous.co);
    const ispuSO2  = calculateISPUFromSO2(anomalous.so2);
    const ispuO3   = calculateISPUFromO3(anomalous.o3);
    const ispuNO2  = calculateISPUFromNO2(anomalous.no2);

    // Tie-breaker: CO → SO2 → O3 → NO2 → PM2.5 → PM10
    const ordered = [
      { name: 'co',   value: ispuCO   },
      { name: 'so2',  value: ispuSO2  },
      { name: 'o3',   value: ispuO3   },
      { name: 'no2',  value: ispuNO2  },
      { name: 'pm25', value: ispuPM25 },
      { name: 'pm10', value: ispuPM10 },
    ];

    finalAQI = Math.max(...ordered.map((c) => c.value));
    dominant = ordered.find((c) => c.value === finalAQI).name;

    if (finalAQI <= 50) finalCategory = 'baik';
    else if (finalAQI <= 100) finalCategory = 'sedang';
    else if (finalAQI <= 200) finalCategory = 'tidak_sehat';
    else if (finalAQI <= 300) finalCategory = 'sangat_tidak_sehat';
    else finalCategory = 'berbahaya';
  }

  const sensorData = {
    deviceId,
    timestamp: now.toISOString(),
    block30: current30SecondBlock,
    secondsInBlock: second % 30,
    updateCounter: state.updateCounter,
    aqi: finalAQI,
    dominant,
    pm25: parseFloat(anomalous.pm25.toFixed(1)),
    pm10: parseFloat(anomalous.pm10.toFixed(1)),
    co: Math.round(anomalous.co),
    so2: parseFloat(anomalous.so2.toFixed(1)),
    o3: parseFloat(anomalous.o3.toFixed(1)),
    no2: parseFloat(anomalous.no2.toFixed(1)),
    temperature: parseFloat(anomalous.temperature.toFixed(1)),
    humidity: Math.round(anomalous.humidity),
    category: finalCategory,
    roomType: ['Kelas', 'Laboratorium', 'Perpustakaan', 'Aula'][state.roomType],
    timeSlot: slot,
    factors: { hour, minute, second },
    isAnomaly: anomalous.isAnomaly,
    anomalyType: anomalous.anomalyType,
  };

  secondDataCache.set(deviceId, {
    block30: current30SecondBlock,
    data: sensorData,
    timestamp: now,
  });

  if (anomalous.isAnomaly) {
    console.log(
      `🚨 ${deviceId} ANOMALY (${anomalous.anomalyType}): AQI=${finalAQI} (${finalCategory}), PM2.5=${sensorData.pm25}, dominant=${dominant}`
    );
  } else {
    console.log(
      `🔄 ${deviceId} [${slot}] AQI=${finalAQI} (${dominant}), PM2.5=${sensorData.pm25}, PM10=${sensorData.pm10}, CO=${sensorData.co}, SO2=${sensorData.so2}, O3=${sensorData.o3}, NO2=${sensorData.no2}`
    );
  }

  return sensorData;
}

function filterSensorData(data, fieldsQuery, excludeQuery) {
  const allFields = ['pm25','pm10','co','so2','o3','no2','temperature','humidity'];

  let selected;

  // ✅ Gunakan !== undefined agar `?fields=` (string kosong) tetap dianggap filter
  if (fieldsQuery !== undefined) {
    selected = new Set(
      fieldsQuery.split(',').map((f) => f.trim()).filter(Boolean)
    );
  } else if (excludeQuery !== undefined) {
    const excluded = new Set(
      excludeQuery.split(',').map((f) => f.trim()).filter(Boolean)
    );
    selected = new Set(allFields.filter((f) => !excluded.has(f)));
  } else {
    selected = new Set(allFields);
  }

  const result = { ...data };
  allFields.forEach((f) => {
    if (!selected.has(f)) result[f] = null;
  });

  // Recompute AQI dari field yang tersisa
  const has = (v) => typeof v === 'number' && !isNaN(v) && v >= 0;
  const ispus = [];
  if (has(result.pm25)) ispus.push({ name: 'pm25', value: calculateISPUFromPM25(result.pm25) });
  if (has(result.pm10)) ispus.push({ name: 'pm10', value: calculateISPUFromPM10(result.pm10) });
  if (has(result.co))   ispus.push({ name: 'co',   value: calculateISPUFromCO(result.co)   });
  if (has(result.so2))  ispus.push({ name: 'so2',  value: calculateISPUFromSO2(result.so2)  });
  if (has(result.o3))   ispus.push({ name: 'o3',   value: calculateISPUFromO3(result.o3)   });
  if (has(result.no2))  ispus.push({ name: 'no2',  value: calculateISPUFromNO2(result.no2)  });

  if (ispus.length === 0) {
    result.aqi = null;
    result.dominant = null;
    result.category = 'no_data';
  } else {
    const order = ['co', 'so2', 'o3', 'no2', 'pm25', 'pm10'];
    ispus.sort((a, b) => order.indexOf(a.name) - order.indexOf(b.name));
    const maxVal = Math.max(...ispus.map((i) => i.value));
    const dom = ispus.find((i) => i.value === maxVal);
    result.aqi = maxVal;
    result.dominant = dom.name;
    if (maxVal <= 50) result.category = 'baik';
    else if (maxVal <= 100) result.category = 'sedang';
    else if (maxVal <= 200) result.category = 'tidak_sehat';
    else if (maxVal <= 300) result.category = 'sangat_tidak_sehat';
    else result.category = 'berbahaya';
  }

  return result;
}

// Clean old cache setiap 30 detik
setInterval(() => {
  const now = Date.now();
  const currentBlock = Math.floor(now / (30 * 1000));

  for (const [deviceId, cache] of secondDataCache.entries()) {
    if (cache.block30 < currentBlock - 3) {
      secondDataCache.delete(deviceId);
    }
  }
}, 30000);

function setCorsHeaders(res) {
  res.setHeader('Access-Control-Allow-Origin', '*');
  res.setHeader('Access-Control-Allow-Methods', 'GET, POST, PUT, DELETE, OPTIONS');
  res.setHeader('Access-Control-Allow-Headers', 'Content-Type');
}

function sendJson(res, statusCode, data) {
  res.writeHead(statusCode, {
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*'
  });
  res.end(JSON.stringify(data, null, 2));
}

const server = http.createServer((req, res) => {
  const parsedUrl = url.parse(req.url, true);
  const pathname = parsedUrl.pathname;
  const method = req.method;

  const now = new Date();
  console.log(`[${now.getHours().toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${now.getMinutes().toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${now.getSeconds().toString().padStart(2, '0')}] ${method} ${pathname}`);

  setCorsHeaders(res);

  if (method === 'OPTIONS') {
    res.writeHead(200);
    res.end();
    return;
  }

  let body = '';
  req.on('data', chunk => { body += chunk.toString(); });

  req.on('end', () => {
    let requestData = {};
    if (body && req.headers['content-type'] === 'application/json') {
      try {
        requestData = JSON.parse(body);
      } catch (e) {
        return sendJson(res, 400, { success: false, message: 'Invalid JSON' });
      }
    }

    try {
      if (pathname === '/health' && (method === 'GET' || method === 'HEAD')) {
        const now = new Date();
        const currentSecond = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
        const current30SecondBlock = Math.floor(currentSecond / 30);
        const nextChangeInSeconds = 30 - (now.getSeconds() % 30);

        sendJson(res, 200, {
          status: 'ok',
          service: 'UNILA AQI IoT Simulator - 30 SECOND UPDATES',
          version: '7.0.0',
          currentTime: now.toISOString(),
          current30SecondBlock,
          secondsInCurrentBlock: now.getSeconds() % 30,
          nextUpdateIn: nextChangeInSeconds,
          devices: registeredDevices.size,
          cacheSize: secondDataCache.size,
          features: [
            '30-second updates',
            'Predictable patterns',
            'Anomaly simulation (5% spike, 3% error)',
            'Room type variations',
            'Time-of-day patterns',
            'Full ISPU parameters (PM2.5, PM10, CO, SO2, O3, NO2)',
            'Dominant pollutant detection'
          ]
        });
        return;
      }

      if (pathname === '/register' && method === 'POST') {
        const { deviceId, deviceName, location, roomType } = requestData;

        if (!deviceId) {
          return sendJson(res, 400, { success: false, message: 'deviceId is required' });
        }

        let roomTypeNum = 0;
        if (roomType === 'laboratory' || roomType === 'lab') roomTypeNum = 1;
        else if (roomType === 'library') roomTypeNum = 2;
        else if (roomType === 'auditorium' || roomType === 'hall') roomTypeNum = 3;

        registeredDevices.set(deviceId, {
          deviceId,
          deviceName: deviceName || `Device-${deviceId.substring(0, 8)}`,
          location: location || 'UNILA Campus',
          roomType: roomTypeNum,
          registeredAt: new Date(),
          lastSeen: new Date(),
          status: 'active',
          updatePattern: '30-second-based'
        });

        console.log(`✅ Device registered: ${deviceId}`);

        sendJson(res, 200, {
          success: true,
          message: 'Device registered successfully',
          device: registeredDevices.get(deviceId),
        });
        return;
      }

      if (pathname.startsWith('/data/') && method === 'GET') {
        const deviceId = pathname.split('/')[2];

        if (!deviceId) {
          return sendJson(res, 400, { success: false, message: 'Device ID is required' });
        }

        // ---- Baca query param DULU ----
        const fieldsQuery  = parsedUrl.query.fields;
        const excludeQuery = parsedUrl.query.exclude;

        // ✅ Deteksi keberadaan query KEY, bukan truthiness value
        const hasFieldsFilter  = fieldsQuery !== undefined;
        const hasExcludeFilter = excludeQuery !== undefined;

        // ... auto-register, generate ...

        let sensorData = generate30SecondBasedData(deviceId);

        // ---- Filter SETELAH generate ----
        if (hasFieldsFilter || hasExcludeFilter) {
          sensorData = filterSensorData(sensorData, fieldsQuery, excludeQuery);
          console.log(
            `🔎 Filtered ${deviceId}: fields=${JSON.stringify(fieldsQuery)} exclude=${JSON.stringify(excludeQuery)} → AQI=${sensorData.aqi}, dominant=${sensorData.dominant}`
          );
        }

        // ---- 5. Bangun response (variabel `now` dst. seperti sebelumnya) ----
        const now = new Date();
        const secondsRemaining = 30 - (now.getSeconds() % 30);

        const response = {
          success: true,
          message: 'Sensor data (updates every 30 seconds)',
          device: registeredDevices.get(deviceId),
          data: sensorData,
          metadata: {
            source: 'UNILA AQI IoT Simulator - 30 SECOND UPDATES',
            updateFrequency: 'Every 30 seconds',
            currentBlock: sensorData.block30,
            secondsInCurrentBlock: sensorData.secondsInBlock,
            nextUpdateInSeconds: secondsRemaining,
            isAnomaly: sensorData.isAnomaly,
            anomalyType: sensorData.anomalyType,
             appliedFilter: {
              fields: hasFieldsFilter ? fieldsQuery : null,
              exclude: hasExcludeFilter ? excludeQuery : null,
            },
            units: {
              pm25: 'μg/m³',
              pm10: 'μg/m³',
              co: 'μg/m³',
              so2: 'μg/m³',
              o3: 'μg/m³',
              no2: 'μg/m³',
              temperature: '°C',
              humidity: '%'
            }
          }
        };

        sendJson(res, 200, response);
        return;
      }

      if (pathname.startsWith('/status/') && method === 'GET') {
        const deviceId = pathname.split('/')[2];

        if (!deviceId) {
          return sendJson(res, 400, { success: false, message: 'Device ID is required' });
        }

        const deviceInfo = registeredDevices.get(deviceId);
        if (!deviceInfo) {
          return sendJson(res, 404, { success: false, message: 'Device not found' });
        }

        const now = new Date();
        const lastSeen = new Date(deviceInfo.lastSeen);
        const secondsSinceLastSeen = (now - lastSeen) / 1000;
        const currentSecond = Math.floor(now.getTime() / 1000);
        const current30SecondBlock = Math.floor(currentSecond / 30);
        const secondsRemaining = 30 - (now.getSeconds() % 30);

        const state = deviceStates.get(deviceId);
        const cache = secondDataCache.get(deviceId);

        sendJson(res, 200, {
          success: true,
          device: deviceInfo,
          status: {
            online: secondsSinceLastSeen < 120,
            lastSeen: deviceInfo.lastSeen,
            secondsSinceLastSeen: Math.round(secondsSinceLastSeen),
            current30SecondBlock,
            secondsInCurrentBlock: now.getSeconds() % 30,
            nextUpdateInSeconds: secondsRemaining,
            hasCachedData: !!cache,
            cachedBlock: cache ? cache.block30 : null,
            stateInfo: state ? {
              roomType: ['Kelas', 'Laboratorium', 'Perpustakaan', 'Aula'][state.roomType],
              updateCounter: state.updateCounter,
            } : 'Not initialized'
          }
        });
        return;
      }

      if (pathname.startsWith('/force-update/') && method === 'POST') {
        const deviceId = pathname.split('/')[2];

        if (!deviceId) {
          return sendJson(res, 400, { success: false, message: 'Device ID is required' });
        }

        secondDataCache.delete(deviceId);

        if (deviceStates.has(deviceId)) {
          const state = deviceStates.get(deviceId);
          state.last30SecondBlock = -1;
        }

        console.log(`🔄 Force update triggered for ${deviceId}`);

        sendJson(res, 200, {
          success: true,
          message: 'Force update triggered',
          deviceId,
          note: 'Next request will generate new 30-second-based data'
        });
        return;
      }

      if (pathname.startsWith('/set-base/') && method === 'POST') {
        const deviceId = pathname.split('/')[2];
        const { pm25, pm10, co, so2, o3, no2, temperature, humidity, roomType } = requestData;

        if (!deviceId) {
          return sendJson(res, 400, { success: false, message: 'Device ID is required' });
        }

        if (!deviceStates.has(deviceId)) {
          deviceStates.set(deviceId, {
            basePM25: 20, basePM10: 40, baseCO: 1500,
            baseSO2: 40, baseO3: 60, baseNO2: 50,
            baseTemp: 23, baseHumidity: 50, roomType: 0,
            trendPM25: 0, trendCO: 0, last30SecondBlock: -1, updateCounter: 0
          });
        }

        const state = deviceStates.get(deviceId);

        if (pm25 !== undefined) state.basePM25 = pm25;
        if (pm10 !== undefined) state.basePM10 = pm10;
        if (co !== undefined) state.baseCO = co;
        if (so2 !== undefined) state.baseSO2 = so2;
        if (o3 !== undefined) state.baseO3 = o3;
        if (no2 !== undefined) state.baseNO2 = no2;
        if (temperature !== undefined) state.baseTemp = temperature;
        if (humidity !== undefined) state.baseHumidity = humidity;
        if (roomType !== undefined) {
          if (roomType === 'laboratory' || roomType === 'lab') state.roomType = 1;
          else if (roomType === 'library') state.roomType = 2;
          else if (roomType === 'auditorium' || roomType === 'hall') state.roomType = 3;
          else state.roomType = 0;
        }

        secondDataCache.delete(deviceId);

        sendJson(res, 200, {
          success: true,
          message: 'Custom base values set',
          deviceId,
          values: {
            basePM25: state.basePM25,
            basePM10: state.basePM10,
            baseCO: state.baseCO,
            baseSO2: state.baseSO2,
            baseO3: state.baseO3,
            baseNO2: state.baseNO2,
            baseTemp: state.baseTemp,
            baseHumidity: state.baseHumidity,
            roomType: ['Kelas', 'Laboratorium', 'Perpustakaan', 'Aula'][state.roomType]
          }
        });
        return;
      }

      if (pathname.startsWith('/history/') && method === 'GET') {
        const deviceId = pathname.split('/')[2];
        const blocks = parseInt(parsedUrl.query.blocks) || 60;

        if (!deviceId) {
          return sendJson(res, 400, { success: false, message: 'Device ID is required' });
        }

        const now = new Date();
        const currentSecond = Math.floor(now.getTime() / 1000);
        const currentBlock = Math.floor(currentSecond / 30);
        const history = [];

        if (deviceStates.has(deviceId)) {
          const state = deviceStates.get(deviceId);

          for (let i = blocks; i >= 0; i--) {
            const historyBlock = currentBlock - i;
            const historyTime = new Date(now.getTime() - (i * 30 * 1000));

            const updateOffset = state.updateCounter - i;
            const hour = historyTime.getHours();
            const minute = historyTime.getMinutes();

            let timeFactor = 1.0;
            if (hour >= 7 && hour <= 18) timeFactor = 1.2;

            const pm25 = state.basePM25 * timeFactor * (1 + Math.sin(updateOffset * 0.2) * 0.2);
            const pm10 = pm25 * 2;
            const co = state.baseCO * timeFactor;
            const so2 = (state.baseSO2 ?? 40) * timeFactor;
            const o3 = (state.baseO3 ?? 60) * timeFactor;
            const no2 = (state.baseNO2 ?? 50) * timeFactor;

            // AQI dihitung dari semua parameter (konsisten dgn backend)
            const ispuPM25 = calculateISPUFromPM25(pm25);
            const ispuPM10 = calculateISPUFromPM10(pm10);
            const ispuCO   = calculateISPUFromCO(co);
            const ispuSO2  = calculateISPUFromSO2(so2);
            const ispuO3   = calculateISPUFromO3(o3);
            const ispuNO2  = calculateISPUFromNO2(no2);
            const aqi = Math.min(500, Math.max(ispuPM25, ispuPM10, ispuCO, ispuSO2, ispuO3, ispuNO2));

            history.push({
              timestamp: historyTime.toISOString(),
              block: historyBlock,
              aqi,
              pm25: parseFloat(pm25.toFixed(1)),
              pm10: parseFloat(pm10.toFixed(1)),
              co: Math.round(co),
              so2: parseFloat(so2.toFixed(1)),
              o3: parseFloat(o3.toFixed(1)),
              no2: parseFloat(no2.toFixed(1)),
              temperature: state.baseTemp + Math.sin(hour * 0.2618 + minute * 0.00436) * 3,
              humidity: state.baseHumidity + Math.cos(hour * 0.2618 + minute * 0.00436) * 10
            });
          }
        }

        sendJson(res, 200, {
          success: true,
          deviceId,
          period: `${blocks * 30} seconds (${blocks} blocks)`,
          currentBlock,
          count: history.length,
          data: history
        });
        return;
      }

      if (pathname === '/devices' && method === 'GET') {
        const devices = Array.from(registeredDevices.values());
        const now = new Date();
        const currentSecond = Math.floor(now.getTime() / 1000);
        const currentBlock = Math.floor(currentSecond / 30);
        const secondsRemaining = 30 - (now.getSeconds() % 30);

        const devicesWithInfo = devices.map(device => {
          const lastSeen = new Date(device.lastSeen);
          const secondsSinceLastSeen = (now - lastSeen) / 1000;
          const state = deviceStates.get(device.deviceId);
          const cache = secondDataCache.get(device.deviceId);

          return {
            ...device,
            roomTypeName: ['Kelas', 'Laboratorium', 'Perpustakaan', 'Aula'][device.roomType || 0],
            status: secondsSinceLastSeen < 120 ? 'online' : 'offline',
            secondsSinceLastSeen: Math.round(secondsSinceLastSeen),
            hasState: !!state,
            updateCounter: state ? state.updateCounter : 0,
            cachedData: !!cache,
            cachedAtBlock: cache ? cache.block30 : null,
            currentBlock,
            nextUpdateIn: secondsRemaining
          };
        });

        sendJson(res, 200, {
          success: true,
          currentBlock,
          currentTime: now.toISOString(),
          secondsInCurrentBlock: now.getSeconds() % 30,
          nextUpdateInSeconds: secondsRemaining,
          totalDevices: devices.length,
          onlineDevices: devicesWithInfo.filter(d => d.status === 'online').length,
          devices: devicesWithInfo
        });
        return;
      }

      sendJson(res, 404, {
        success: false,
        message: 'Endpoint not found',
        path: pathname
      });

    } catch (error) {
      console.error('❌ Server error:', error);
      sendJson(res, 500, {
        success: false,
        message: 'Internal server error',
        error: error.message
      });
    }
  });
});

// ============================================================
// DEPLOY RENDER: bind 0.0.0.0 & gunakan process.env.PORT
// ============================================================
const PORT = process.env.PORT || process.env.IOT_PORT || 3002;
const HOST = '0.0.0.0'; // ✅ WAJIB untuk Render / Docker / VPS

server.listen(PORT, HOST, () => {
  console.log('🚀 UNILA AQI IoT Simulator - 30 SECOND UPDATES');
  console.log(`📍 URL: http://${HOST}:${PORT}`);
  console.log(`📡 Health Check: http://${HOST}:${PORT}/health`);

  console.log('\n⏰ FEATURE HIGHLIGHTS:');
  console.log('   ✅ Data BERUBAH SETIAP 30 DETIK');
  console.log('   ✅ Pola berdasarkan waktu (jam, hari)');
  console.log('   ✅ ANOMALY: 5% spike (8-17x), 3% sensor error');
  console.log('   ✅ Cache per 30 detik');
  console.log('   ✅ Room type variations');
  console.log('   ✅ EMA smoothing (alpha=0.4)');
  console.log('   ✅ ISPU untuk 6 parameter: PM2.5, PM10, CO, SO2, O3, NO2');
  console.log('   ✅ Dominant pollutant detection');

  console.log('\n💡 Contoh untuk Aplikasi AQI:');
  console.log(`  Endpoint: http://${HOST}:${PORT}/data/kelas-h101`);
  console.log(`  Data akan berubah setiap 30 detik`);
  console.log(`  Sekitar 5% request akan menghasilkan AQI 100-500 (spike)`);
  console.log(`  Sekitar 3% request akan menghasilkan AQI=-1 (sensor error)`);
});
